A unified variation-conditioned compatibility score is further formulated to fuse distributional similarity and soft variation aggregation, guiding voxel embeddings to align with both global organ identity and local anatomical variations. VCDP is attached to decoder features during training and removed during inference, introducing no additional inference cost. Experiments on multi-organ segmentation benchmarks show that VCDP improves most evaluated baselines, particularly for small, ambiguous, and highly variable organs. Our anonymous code is released at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/VCDP_code-41ED.

</details>


### [98] [Two-Stage Multi-Modal Fusion with Adaptive Alignment for Action Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07438)
*Kanglei Zhou,Ruizhi Cai,Xinning Wang,Yijian Zheng,Liyuan Wang,Jianguo Li,Xiaohui Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DualAlign，一种两阶段多模态融合框架，通过自适应对齐解决动作质量评估中跨模态错位和标注稀缺问题，在MM-JDM等基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作质量评估方法中，单模态方法难以捕捉细微动作线索，多模态方法面临跨模态错位导致融合不稳定，以及多模态标注成本高、数据集多样性受限两大挑战。

Method: 提出DualAlign两阶段框架：第一阶段通过最大化RGB视频、光流和骨骼模态间的共享结构信息构建一致的视觉表征；第二阶段在视觉稳定后引入文本语义，使高层描述补充而非扭曲视觉流形。同时构建了包含四种模态且自然存在噪声、类别不平衡和标签稀缺的MM-JDM数据集。

Result: 在MM-JDM上相比SOTA提升21.16%平均相关性，在RG和Fis-V基准上分别提升3.53%和5.95%，且在模态缺失和标签稀缺条件下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DualAlign通过两阶段自适应对齐策略有效解决了多模态融合中的跨模态错位问题，在多个基准上取得最优性能，并具有较强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Action Quality Assessment (AQA) aims to evaluate how well a person performs a movement, which is essential in applications such as sports scoring, skill assessment, and healthcare. However, unimodal approaches often struggle to capture subtle cues of movement quality in real-world settings. Although multi-modal inputs provide complementary information, existing methods still face two major challenges: heterogeneous modalities often lead to cross-modal misalignment and unstable fusion, and reliable multi-modal annotation is costly, resulting in limited dataset diversity. To address these challenges, we propose DualAlign, a two-stage multi-modal fusion framework with adaptive alignment. The framework first constructs a coherent visual representation by maximizing shared structural information across RGB video, optical flow, and skeleton modalities. Textual semantics are then incorporated after visual stabilization, allowing high-level descriptions to complement rather than distort the underlying visual manifold. To evaluate the framework under realistic multi-modal conditions, we introduce MM--JDM, a movement-quality assessment dataset integrating RGB videos, optical flow, skeleton sequences, and structured text. MM--JDM naturally exhibits modality noise, class imbalance, and label scarcity, making it a challenging benchmark for studying multi-modal fusion and alignment. Extensive experiments show that DualAlign improves average correlation on MM--JDM by 21.16% over the state-of-the-art methods and achieves gains of 3.53% and 5.95% on the RG and Fis-V benchmarks, respectively. DualAlign also remains robust under missing-modality and label-scarce conditions.

</details>


### [99] [A Theory of Contrastive Learning with Natural Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07470)
*Antonio Torralba,Yair Weiss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析证明，在具有平稳统计特性的图像数据集上，对比学习的最优表示可以通过第一层为正弦波滤波器、后接逐点非线性、全局平均池化和部分白化线性层的CNN实现，并给出了基于功率谱的注水算法来计算滤波器参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究简单图像和增强下的对比学习为何能为下游任务产生有用表示，从理论上揭示其内在机制。

Method: 理论分析最优表示在对比损失下的解析形式，针对平稳统计图像数据集和基本增强方式，推导出CNN最优结构（正弦波滤波器+逐点非线性+全局平均池化+部分白化线性层），并给出基于数据集功率谱的注水算法。

Result: 最优CNN第一层滤波器为正弦波，其频率和权重可由注水算法从数据功率谱计算得出；更复杂增强下最优权重仍为正弦波；实验验证SGD训练可学习到正弦波和部分白化行为。

Conclusion: 对比学习的有效性与数据的频域统计特性密切相关，最优表示可通过特定结构CNN和功率谱注水算法显式构造，为理解自监督学习提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Why does contrastive learning with simple images and augmentations yield useful representations for downstream tasks? We address this question by analytically computing the optimal representation in terms of a contrastive loss for a range of basic augmentations and any image dataset with stationary statistics. We show that for certain augmentations the optimum can be attained by a CNN whose first layer filters are sinusoids, followed by a pointwise nonlinearity, global average pooling, and a final linear layer that performs partial whitening. We also show that the optimal weights in such CNNs for more complicated augmentations are still sinusoids. The frequencies of the sinusoids and their weights can be computed using a simple waterfilling algorithm given the dataset's expected power spectrum. Experiments with different image datasets and augmentations show that such CNNs trained with SGD empirically learn sinusoids in their first layer and to perform partial whitening

</details>


### [100] [Discovering Geometric Biases in 3D Face Reconstruction: A Curvature-Aware Spectral Framework for Fairness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07486)
*Veronika Shilova,Emmanuel Malherbe,Giovanni Palma,Panagiotis-Alexandros Bokaris,Laurent Risser,Jean-Michel Loubes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于曲面曲率的框架，利用Laplace-Beltrami算子生成高分辨率曲率误差图，用于分析3DMM重建中的形态偏差，发现并量化了年龄、性别和种族相关的系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DMM模型因训练数据有限而存在形态偏差，传统欧氏距离评估指标无法捕捉局部拓扑和起伏等细微曲面差异，缺乏对重建偏差的精细化和几何上有意义的评估方法。

Method: 利用Laplace-Beltrami算子（LBO）生成高分辨率曲率误差图，从曲面曲率角度分析3DMM重建结果；基于此推导出新的误差度量，并通过用户研究验证其与人类感知的相关性高于传统方法。

Result: 新提出的曲率误差度量与人类感知的相关性显著高于传统方法；在多个3DMM基和拟合算法上发现系统性年龄相关偏差，并初步证据表明存在性别和种族偏差。

Conclusion: 未来3D人脸重建研究应采用曲率感知的评估协议以确保几何精度和人口统计学公平性。

Abstract: 3D Morphable Models (3DMMs) remain the standard parametric shape priors for many state-of-the-art 3D face reconstruction algorithms. However, as these models are derived from a finite number of 3D face samples, they inherit the morphological biases of their training data, potentially limiting their generalizability across diverse global populations. In this paper, we propose a novel framework to analyze 3DMM reconstructions through the lens of surface curvature, with the objective to discover, quantify and visualize biases. While standard evaluation metrics often rely on Euclidean distances, our reconstruction error captures subtle surface nuances such as local topology or undulations. To do so, we leverage the Laplace-Beltrami Operator (LBO) to generate high-resolution curvature error maps, providing a localized and geometrically meaningful visualization of discrepancies between ground truth faces and reconstructed meshes. We derive from it an error metric that we validated through a user study, observing a significantly higher correlation to human perception compared to traditional methods. Furthermore, we conduct extensive experiments across several 3DMM bases and fitting algorithms, uncovering systematic age-related biases and providing preliminary evidence of biases associated with gender and ethnicity. Our findings highlight the necessity of adopting curvature-aware evaluation protocols to ensure demographic fairness and geometric precision in future 3D face reconstruction research.

</details>


### [101] [HIVE: Understanding Post-Hallucination Reasoning in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07507)
*Feng He,Zhenting Wang,Qifan Wang,Qiang Guan,Dongfang Liu,Ruixiang Tang,Qiankun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型中的“幻觉后推理”（PHR）阶段，即幻觉语义进入模型推理上下文并影响下游预测的过程。通过引入HIVE评估框架，在9个任务和9个模型上进行实验，发现幻觉描述在视觉语言任务中常能提升准确率，而在纯文本任务中效果有限或不稳定。进一步分析表明，幻觉线索扩大了语义覆盖范围并重塑了推理动态，同时保持稳定的推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注在生成时检测或抑制幻觉，但忽略了幻觉语义进入模型推理上下文后对下游预测的影响。本文旨在系统研究这一“幻觉后推理”阶段，以提升多模态推理系统的可靠性和可解释性。

Method: 提出HIVE（幻觉推理与验证引擎）评估框架，通过可控对比（忠实描述vs幻觉描述），在9个任务和9个模型上进行系统实验，分析幻觉语义对下游推理的影响。

Result: 幻觉描述在视觉语言任务中常能提升准确率；纯文本任务中效果有限或不稳定；幻觉线索能扩大语义覆盖范围、重塑推理动态，同时保持稳定的推理过程。

Conclusion: 幻觉语义一旦进入模型的推理上下文，可能影响下游推理。理解这一“幻觉后”阶段对于改进多模态推理系统的可靠性和可解释性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Hallucinations in vision language models (VLMs) are commonly treated as semantic errors, yet they often arise from partial or ambiguous visual evidence. Prior work mainly focuses on detecting or suppressing hallucinations at generation time, leaving the subsequent reasoning stage largely unexplored. In this work, we study Post Hallucination Reasoning (PHR), the stage in which hallucinated semantics enter the model's inference context and influence downstream predictions. To systematically investigate PHR, we introduce HIVE, Hallucination Inference and Verification Engine, an evaluation infrastructure that enables controlled comparisons between faithful and hallucinated captions. Across nine tasks and nine models, we observe structured modality dependent patterns: hallucinated captions often improve accuracy on vision language tasks, while text only tasks exhibit limited or unstable effects. Further analyses show that hallucinated cues broaden semantic coverage and reshape reasoning dynamics while preserving stable inference. These findings highlight that hallucinated semantics may influence downstream reasoning once they enter the model's inference context. Understanding this post hallucination stage is important for improving the reliability and interpretability of multimodal reasoning systems. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/hefengcs/HIVE.

</details>


### [102] [Learning to Unify Deformable Shape and Texture Representations for Cardiac Video Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07518)
*Tonmoy Hossain,Miaomiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种心脏视频分类模型，首次在可变形形状与图像纹理的融合空间中学习时序特征，通过双向交叉注意力机制实现模态间的自适应加权，并在心脏磁共振视频数据集上取得了最优分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度网络对可变形形状与纹理特征仅做简单拼接，未能充分利用两者的互补性，也无法学习跨模态特征依赖关系；同时对所有时间点施加均匀注意力，忽略了不同心脏相位在诊断中的重要程度差异。

Method: 提出一种心脏视频分类模型，在潜在空间中设计双向交叉注意力机制，融合可变形形状与图像纹理的潜在特征，使两种模态根据时空对应关系自适应地为对方加权；并动态调整各心脏相位上形状与纹理表示的贡献。

Result: 在电影心脏磁共振（CMR）视频数据集上取得了最先进的分类性能，并通过注意力机制实现了更高的可解释性，能够识别出诊断关键的心脏相位及各模态的贡献。

Conclusion: 在可变形形状与图像纹理的融合空间中学习时序特征，结合双向交叉注意力与动态相位加权，能够有效提升心脏视频分类性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Deformable shape representations have proven to be robust complements to texture features in cardiac image classification, offering geometric priors that are invariant to imaging artifacts and intensity variations. However, existing deep networks perform simple concatenation to combine these distinct feature representations, which neither fully exploits their complementary nature nor learns cross-modal feature dependencies. Furthermore, this results in uniform attention across all timepoints; hence ignoring the varying diagnostic importance across the cardiac phases. In this paper, we propose a novel cardiac video classification model that, for the first time, learns temporal features in an integrated space of deformable shape and image texture representations. In particular, we design a bi-directional cross-attention in the latent space to fuse latent deformable shape and image features, allowing each modality to adaptively weight the other based on spatio-temporal correspondence. In contrast to current methods that apply uniform weighting across all the cardiac phases, our approach learns to dynamically adjust the contributions of shape and texture representations, derived from images, over time. We demonstrate state-of-the-art classification performance on a cine cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) video dataset, achieving improved interpretability from attention mechanisms that identify diagnostically critical cardiac phases and modality contributions.

</details>


### [103] [Context-Aware Slum Mapping in Sub-Saharan Africa Using Sentinel-1 Texture and Local Climate Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07532)
*Peterson Chepkilot,Babak Memar,Paolo Gamba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合Sentinel-2光学影像与Sentinel-1 SAR数据的上下文感知框架，通过三层次SAR集成策略（定标后向散射、GLCM纹理、物理引导特征）显著提升了撒哈拉以南非洲城市非正规住区（LCZ 7）的识别精度，总体精度达0.81以上，并将LCZ 3与LCZ 7的混淆降至7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学影像在撒哈拉以南非洲城市中难以区分非正规住区（LCZ 7）与光谱相似的正式紧凑低层住宅区（LCZ 3），现有方法精度不足且跨季节、跨城市泛化能力有限。

Method: 采用三层次SAR集成策略：（1）定标后向散射，（2）GLCM纹理特征，（3）物理引导的特征工程（捕捉高结构无序性和弱雷达回波）。结合Sentinel-2光谱特征与Sentinel-1结构信息，基于适应性LCZ分类体系，在内罗毕和埃尔多雷特进行分层留出验证和季节感知消融研究。

Result: 光学-SAR模型在旱季和雨季总体精度分别达0.816和0.807，显著优于WUDAPT基线（0.704）；LCZ 3与LCZ 7的混淆降低至7%；SAR纹理特征对LCZ 7检测贡献最大；跨季节表现稳定，但跨城市迁移能力有限。

Conclusion: SAR衍生特征的融入能在数据稀缺环境中实现城市形态映射的跨季节一致性提升，但跨城市迁移仍需本地化适应策略。

Abstract: Accurate mapping of informal settlements remains a major challenge in Sub-Saharan African (SSA) cities because optical imagery often fails to distinguish Informal Settlements (defined here as LCZ 7) from spectrally similar formal Compact Low-Rise areas (LCZ 3). This study presents a context-aware, reproducible Optical-SAR framework that improves informal settlement delineation using Sentinel-2 spectral features and Sentinel-1 structural information within an adapted Local Climate Zone (LCZ) taxonomy. We implement a three-tier SAR integration strategy: calibrated backscatter, GLCM textures, and a physics-guided feature engineered to capture the high structural disorder and weak radar return characteristic of SSA informal settlements. Using reference data across Nairobi and Eldoret (Kenya), we evaluate performance via a stratified hold-out protocol and a season-aware ablation study. Results show that SAR textures provide the dominant performance gain for LCZ 7 detection. The Optical-SAR model achieves overall accuracy of 0.816 (dry) and 0.807 (wet), significantly outperforming the WUDAPT baseline (OA 0.704) and reducing the critical LCZ 3 - LCZ 7 confusion to 7%. Seasonal analysis indicates that while optical-only separability varies with phenology, SAR-derived textures stabilize informal settlement mapping across seasons. These findings demonstrate that the incorporation of SAR-derived features yields consistent improvements for urban morphology mapping in data-scarce environments across seasons and across the evaluated source cities, while cross-city transfer remains limited without local adaptation strategies.

</details>


### [104] [Infinite Worlds with Versatile Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07534)
*Zelin Gao,Qiuyu Wang,Jiapeng Zhu,Jingye Chen,Zichen Liu,Qingyan Bai,Jiahao Wang,Yufeng Yuan,Hanlin Wang,Yichong Lu,Ka Leong Cheng,Haojie Zhang,Jian Gao,Tianrui Feng,Yuzheng Liu,Yao Yao,Yinghao Xu,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Hao Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LingBot-World 2.0 是一个升级后的世界模拟器，支持无限交互、实时60fps 720p视频生成、多样化动作和文本驱动事件，并创新地引入智能体协同架构（飞行员+导演），同时支持多玩家共享体验，提供14B和轻量1.3B两种模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在上一版基础上，进一步提升世界模型的交互时长、实时性、交互多样性，并引入智能体编排机制以增强场景动态生成能力，同时实现多用户共享体验。

Method: (1) 精心设计的因果预训练范式实现无限交互；(2) 从基模型蒸馏实时变体，支持60fps 720p视频；(3) 扩展动作空间（攻击、射箭、施法、射击等）及文本驱动事件；(4) 引入双智能体架构：飞行员智能体规划执行角色行为，导演智能体合成新环境元素。同时开发多玩家共享界面，提供14B主模型和1.3B轻量模型。

Result: 实现无限交互时长、实时60fps 720p视频生成、高度多样化的交互内容，以及多玩家共享沉浸式体验，且1.3B模型可单GPU部署。

Conclusion: LingBot-World 2.0 通过四项升级显著提升了世界模型的交互能力、实时性和多样性，双智能体架构为世界建模提供了新范式，多玩家支持拓展了应用场景。

Abstract: We present LingBot-World 2.0 (also known as LingBot-World-Infinity), an advanced iteration of LingBot-World featuring four distinct upgrades. (1) Our model achieves an unbounded interaction horizon while maintaining consistent output quality, benefiting from a carefully crafted causal pretraining paradigm. (2) Through distilling a real-time variant from the base model, our system guarantees rapid response time, sufficient to drive 720p video streams at 60 fps. (3) Compared to the previous version, this update introduces highly diverse interactive elements, comprising a broader spectrum of actions (e.g., attacking, archery, spell-casting, and shooting) alongside a richer variety of text-driven events. (4) We pioneer the integration of an agentic harness within the domain of world modeling, wherein a pilot agent is tasked with planning and executing character behaviors, while a director agent is responsible for synthesizing novel environmental elements as the scene progresses. Additionally, to facilitate a shared experience, we develop an interface that permits multiple players to simultaneously immerse themselves in this vivid world simulator. We pair our primary 14B model with a lightweight 1.3B counterpart, which supports effortless deployment on a single GPU.

</details>


### [105] [Face-trace: Open-Set Attribution and Progressive Discovery of Synthetic Face Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07545)
*Alessia Infantino,Claudio Schiavella,Irene Amerini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向合成人脸图像的开集源归属流水线，结合已知生成器分类、基于能量的开放集拒绝和未知生成器发现，在WILD数据集上实现了96.73%的闭集准确率、71.25%的开放集平衡准确率，以及0.81的ARI和87.74%的聚类纯度，并支持增量场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成人脸图像源归属方法大多假设闭集场景（所有生成器在训练时已知），但现实中新生成器不断涌现，被拒绝样本应被组织而非简单丢弃。本文旨在解决开集场景下的合成人脸源归属问题。

Method: 采用三阶段流水线：(1) 基于冻结I-JEPA嵌入训练已知生成器分类器；(2) 基于能量的OOD拒绝识别未知生成器样本；(3) 将投影I-JEPA特征与Forensic Self-Descriptors结合表示拒绝样本，通过聚类发现未知生成器分组。同时扩展至增量场景。

Result: WILD数据集上闭集准确率96.73%；开集拒绝平衡准确率71.25%；拒绝样本聚类ARI 0.81、NMI 0.90、纯度87.74%；增量场景最终纯度99.23%；跨数据集实验表明具有一定泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提开集合成人脸源归属流水线能有效处理已知和未知生成器场景，支持增量扩展，但后处理仍有挑战。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative Artificial Intelligence have made synthetic face images increasingly realistic, creating new challenges for multimedia forensics. Source attribution methods should not only identify the generator of an image when the source is known, but also handle samples produced by previously unseen models. However, most existing approaches address synthetic face attribution in a closed-set setting, where all possible generators are available during training. This assumption does not hold in real-world scenarios, where new generators continuously appear and rejected samples should be organized rather than simply discarded. In this work we propose a pipeline for open-set synthetic face source attribution that combines known generator classification, energy-based OOD rejection, and unknown generator discovery. A classifier is trained on known generators using frozen I-JEPA embeddings, while rejected samples are represented by combining projected I-JEPA features with Forensic Self-Descriptors and then clustered to discover groups of unknown generators. We also extend the discovery stage to an incremental scenario, where rejected samples arrive over time. Experiments on the WILD dataset show that the proposed method achieves 96.73% closed-set attribution accuracy. In the open-set setting, energy-based rejection reaches 71.25% balanced accuracy, while rejected samples are clustered into meaningful unknown-generator groups, obtaining an ARI of 0.81, an NMI of 0.90, and an overall clustering purity of 87.74%. In the incremental setting, the discovered generator space is progressively extended while maintaining a final purity of 99.23%. Cross-dataset experiments suggest that the pipeline can operate beyond the original dataset distribution, although post-processing remains challenging.

</details>


### [106] [AA-ViT: Anatomically Aware Vision Transformer with Structural and Frequency Guidance for Contrast Enhanced Brain MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07553)
*Talha Meraj,Tom Flannery,Charlie Cummins,Matt Townend,Thomas C Booth,Peter Crossley,Michael McCann,Ian Overton,Saritha Unnikrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种解剖感知的频域与结构引导视觉Transformer（AA-ViT），用于无对比剂增强MRI合成，在BraTS 2021数据集上优于现有方法，并获得临床专家初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MRI常存在对比度不足和伪影问题，需使用对比剂增强病变可见性，但对比剂在肾功能不全等患者中禁忌使用，因此开发非侵入性的对比增强MRI合成方法具有重要临床意义。

Method: 提出AA-ViT（解剖感知频域与结构引导视觉Transformer），利用预增强MRI模态（T1、T2、FLAIR）进行CEMRI合成，通过频域和结构信息引导增强解剖边界和肿瘤精细结构的保持。

Result: 在BraTS 2021数据集上PSNR和SSIM均优于现有方法；三位神经放射学家和一位神经外科医生对19例随机病例的临床评分为3.94/5（满分5分）。

Conclusion: 所提方法能生成高质量合成后对比增强扫描，可降低扫描成本、缩短成像时间、避免钆对比剂潜在风险，且获得了初步临床验证。

Abstract: Accurate tumour localization and diagnosis is a critical component of clinical care for brain cancers. Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is the most commonly used imaging modality due to its superior soft-tissue contrast. However, standard MRI often exhibits limited contrast and imaging artifacts, which necessitates the use of contrast agents to enhance lesion visibility. The administration of chemical contrast agents is not always feasible and may be contraindicated in patients with renal impairment or other health conditions. As a result, developing accurate and non-invasive contrast enhanced MRI (CEMRI) synthesis methods has clinical importance. In recent years, numerous approaches for CEMRI synthesis have been proposed, predominantly relying on generative artificial intelligence models. While these methods demonstrate promising performance, their dependence on implicit feature learning often limits their ability to preserve anatomical boundaries and tumour-specific fine structures. To address these challenges, we propose an anatomically aware frequency-and-structure-guided vision transformer (AA-ViT), for CEMRI synthesis using pre-contrast MRI modalities (T1, T2, and FLAIR). Experiments on the BraTS 2021 dataset demonstrate that the proposed method preserves anatomical and lesion boundaries, achieving higher PSNR and SSIM than state-of-the-art approaches. Clinical evaluation by three neuroradiologists and a neurosurgeon on 19 randomly selected cases across diverse gliomas yielded a mean score of 3.94/5, providing preliminary clinical validation rarely seen in prior studies. Synthetic post-contrast scans from our model could lower scanning costs, shorten imaging time, and avoid the potential risks of using gadolinium-based contrast agents.

</details>


### [107] [Automatic Echocardiography Segmentation via Transition Probability Correlation for Stable Semantic Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07580)
*Xinran Chen,Xiyuan Wang,Guangquan Zhou,Chuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对超声心动图噪声大、边界模糊等问题，本文提出了STLSF模块（含语义校正与纹理增强）和频率感知去噪预训练方法，构建了兼具局部归纳偏置与长程依赖的卷积网络，在CAMUS和EchoNet-Dynamic数据集上取得了SOTA分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图固有的散斑噪声和低信噪比会导致模糊的语义特征和不完整的边界，严重限制了深度学习模型在复杂临床病例中的分割精度；同时心脏的时序运动对解剖结构识别至关重要。

Method: 设计了STLSF模块，包含基于窗口匹配的语义校正组件和语义引导的纹理增强组件，利用局部转移概率相关性校正语义并通过语义引导的纹理增强缓解纹理不稳定和语义歧义；提出了频率感知去噪预训练方法以适应超声成像模式的固有先验；构建了兼具局部归纳偏置和长程依赖的卷积网络。

Result: 在CAMUS上达到93.87% Dice，HD95为3.29mm；在EchoNet-Dynamic上达到92.62% Dice，HD95为2.73mm，均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的STLSF模块和频率感知去噪预训练方法有效克服了超声心动图质量差带来的纹理不稳定和语义歧义问题，实现了高精度的心脏分割。

Abstract: While echocardiography is essential for cardiovascular diagnosis, inherent speckle noise and low signal-to-noise ratio often lead to ambiguous semantic features and fragmented boundaries. These limitations significantly hinder the segmentation accuracy of deep learning models in complex clinical cases. Moreover, temporal motion of the heart plays a critical role in recognizing anatomical structures. To address these challenges, we designed a STLSF module which comprises a window-matching-based semantic correction component and a semantics-guided texture enhancement component. By leveraging local transition probability correlations to correct semantics and employing semantics-guided texture enhancement, the STLSF module effectively mitigates texture instability and ambiguous semantic interpretations caused by disadvantaged echocardiography quality. Additionally, to facilitate the encoder's adaptation to the intrinsic priors of ultrasound-specific imaging patterns, we propose a frequency-aware denoising pre-training method. The entire work builds a convolution-based network with locality inductive bias and long-range dependencies. Extensive experiments confirm our SOTA performance, achieving 93.87\% Dice on CAMUS and 92.62\% on EchoNet-Dynamic, with respective HD95 values of 3.29mm and 2.73mm.

</details>


### [108] [Cardiac MRI Through-Plane Super-Resolution Guided by Reference and Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07581)
*Shaoming Pan,Chenchuhui Hu,Leon Axel,Meng Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STRMSR框架，利用同一受试者的高分辨率参考视图和中间超分辨率结果作为记忆，通过粗到精的上下文匹配和可学习补丁动态特征聚合模块，实现心脏MRI的参考和记忆引导的跨平面超分辨率重建，在4倍和8倍上采样因子下优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床心脏MRI通常具有高面内分辨率但粗跨平面分辨率，这限制了3D分析和诊断准确性，需要一种有效的跨平面超分辨率方法。

Method: 提出了STRMSR框架，包含：(1) 粗到精的上下文匹配建立低分辨率目标与参考/记忆图像间的鲁棒对应；(2) 可学习的补丁级动态特征聚合模块预测内容自适应混合权重，融合动态信息并抑制不可靠特征传递；(3) 记忆库存储中间SR结果确保切片间一致性。

Result: 在WHS心脏MRI数据集上，分别在正交平面视图和长轴腔视图两种参考协议下进行实验，在4倍和8倍上采样因子下均取得一致优于基线的性能提升。

Conclusion: STRMSR通过引入参考视图和记忆机制有效提升了心脏MRI跨平面超分辨率的重建质量，且在不同参考协议和上采样倍数下均表现稳定。

Abstract: Clinical cardiac MRI is commonly acquired with high in-plane resolution but coarse through-plane resolution to reduce scan time and accommodate breath-hold and cardiac-motion constraints, which limits 3D analysis and diagnostic accuracy. We propose STRMSR, a reference- and memory-guided through-plane super-resolution (SR) framework that reconstructs high-resolution (HR) cardiac volumes by leveraging HR reference views acquired from the same subject and intermediate SR results as the memory. Our method uses coarse-to-fine contextual matching to establish robust correspondence between low-resolution target and reference/memory images under spatial misalignment. A learnable patch-wise dynamic feature aggregation module predicts content-adaptive mixture weights for each local patch, effectively fusing dynamic information while suppressing unreliable feature transfers. The intermediate SR results stored in the memory bank ensure slice-to-slice consistency for the super-resolved 3D volume. Experiments on the WHS cardiac MRI dataset under two reference protocols, orthogonal-plane views and long-axis chamber views, demonstrate consistent improvements over baselines at 4x and 8x upsampling factors.

</details>


### [109] [MedPMC: A Systematic Framework for Scaling High-Fidelity Medical Multimodal Data for Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07673)
*Hyunjae Kim,Dain Kim,Pan Xiao,Serina S. Applebaum,Younjoon Chung,Xuguang Ai,Yu Yin,Roy Jiang,Yuexi Du,Yawen Wei,Yiming Kong,Tuo Guo,Zhiyuan Cao,Mengmeng Du,Yuelei Fu,Yan Hu,Rui Shi,Gui Yang,Kevin W. Jin,Yuntian Liu,Yuxuan Tian,Jonathan Marquez,Zhen Chen,Sheng Zhang,Hoifung Poon,Hua Xu,Jaewoo Kang,Qingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedPMC是一个自动化、持续更新的框架，从PMC文献中提取1100万高质量医学图像-文本对，经训练的多模态模型在26项基准测试和临床验证中显著优于现有方法，证明了高保真文献 curation 对医学多模态基础模型的强化作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学本质上是多模态的，但多模态基础模型的开发受限于大规模高质量临床数据的获取。虽然PMC提供了专家撰写的图像-文本数据来源，但现有PMC衍生数据集在保真度、可复现性和临床验证方面存在局限。

Method: MedPMC框架：从610万篇PMC文章中自动化提取和curate医学图像-文本对，包含初始筛选、多面板图形检测、图形分离、标题分离与对齐、医学图形分类等组件，最终获得1100万对高质量数据。

Result: 组件评估F1达81.4-96.5；人工审核95.3%图像医学相关（对比先前数据集仅19.7%）；CLIP模型零样本AUC提升7.1个百分点；医疗VQA提升1.9-16.9个百分点；临床皮肤科照片检索Recall@5提升11.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 高保真的文献curation能够在基准测试和临床环境中显著增强医学多模态基础模型的性能。

Abstract: Medicine is inherently multimodal, requiring clinicians to synthesize information across diverse data streams. Yet the development of multimodal foundation models is constrained by limited access to large-scale, high-quality clinical data. Although PubMed Central (PMC) offers a complementary source of expert-authored image-text data, existing PMC-derived resources remain limited in fidelity, reproducibility, and clinical validation. We introduce MedPMC, an automated, continuously updatable framework that transforms permissively licensed literature into high-fidelity infrastructure for medical multimodal models. Applied to 6.1 million PMC articles, MedPMC curated 11 million medical image-text pairs. Component evaluations showed strong performance for initial screening (F1 = 93.2), multi-panel figure detection (F1 = 96.5), figure separation (mAP = 89.8), caption separation and alignment (F1 = 81.4; ROUGE-L = 85.3), and medical figure classification (F1 = 96.5). Manual review by five annotators, three with medical training, found 95.3% of MedPMC images medically relevant, versus 19.7% in a prior PMC-derived dataset. Across 26 benchmarks spanning 11 specialties, a MedPMC-trained CLIP-style model improved average zero-shot AUC by 7.1 percentage points over the strongest architecture-matched biomedical CLIP baseline despite using fewer than half as many image-text pairs. As the vision encoder in a multimodal large language model, it improved medical visual question-answering by 1.9 and 16.9 percentage points across two benchmarks. In 10,524 Yale New Haven Health System dermatology photographs, it improved morphology-to-image retrieval Recall@5 by 11.7 percentage points. These findings show that high-fidelity literature curation strengthens medical multimodal foundation models across benchmark and clinical settings. We publicly release the framework, corpus, benchmarks, and pretrained models.

</details>


### [110] [Scaling Mixture-of-Experts Video Pretraining for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07675)
*Shuailei Ma,Jiaqi Liao,Xinyang Wang,Jingjing Wang,Chaoran Feng,Zijing Hu,Chong Bao,Zichen Xi,Yuqi Gan,Weisen Wang,Yanhong Zeng,Qin Zhao,Zifan Shi,Wei Wu,Hao Ouyang,Qiuyu Wang,Shangzhan Zhang,Jiahao Shao,Yipengjing Sun,Liangxiao Hu,Lunke Pan,Nan Xue,Kecheng Zheng,Yinghao Xu,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Ka Leong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LingBot-Video 是一个基于 DiT 和 MoE 架构的视频预训练范式，专门为具身智能设计，通过面向机器人的数据增强和多维奖励系统，弥合数字内容创作与物理驱动之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型主要面向内容创作，优先考虑视觉保真度和创造性，忽视了计算效率和物理 realism，导致在机器人控制领域存在领域不匹配问题。

Method: 1) 架构上采用 MoE（混合专家）替代密集模型，实现建模能力与推理效率的更好权衡并实现从零扩展；2) 数据上构建数据画像引擎，用大量面向机器人的视频（操作、导航、第一人称视角）增强标准互联网视频；3) 训练上开发多维奖励系统，强制对齐物理合理性和任务完成度，超越传统的美学、提示遵循和运动一致性标准。

Result: 综合评估验证了 LingBot-Video 作为视频基础模型的性能和效率，是首个大规模开源 MoE 视频基础模型。

Conclusion: LingBot-Video 开创性地弥合了数字创意与物理驱动之间的差距，为具身智能提供更高效的视频预训练范式。

Abstract: Despite the recent promise in robot control, video generative models suffer from a domain mismatch due to their primary focus on content creation. For example, their design inherently prioritizes visual fidelity and creativity over computational efficiency and physical realism. In this work, we present LingBot-Video, a DiT-based video pretraining paradigm specifically tailored for embodied intelligence. From the architecture perspective, we adopt the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), instead of dense, framework to achieve a better trade-off between modeling capacity and inference efficiency, and manage to scale it up from scratch. From the data perspective, we construct a data profiling engine that augments standard internet videos with extensive robot-oriented footage, encompassing manipulation, navigation, and egocentric perspectives, to equip the base model with an intrinsic understanding of actions and world dynamics. From the training perspective, we develop a multi-dimensional reward system to enforce the alignment regarding physical rationality and task completion, going beyond standard criteria such as aesthetics, prompt-following, and motion consistency. Comprehensive evaluations validate its performance and efficiency as a video foundation model. We contribute LingBot-Video as the inaugural large-scale, open-source MoE video foundation model to the community, in a pioneering effort to bridge digital creativity and physical actuation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Blockchain Attacks and Defenses: A Layered and Cross-Domain Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06593)
*Junjie Hu,Na Ruan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对区块链安全研究进行了系统性综述，从四层分层架构（网络层、密码学层、共识层、应用层）和跨域信任边界两个维度，对威胁态势及防御策略进行分类，并探讨了DeFi和跨链互操作兴起后的安全演化、协议组合时的安全假设失效问题以及未来Web3安全研究需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链已从简单分布式账本演变为承载复杂应用逻辑和巨大金融价值的可编程平台，攻击面也随之从基础设施破坏转向可编程经济滥用和跨域漏洞利用。作者旨在系统梳理安全研究演化，回答过去十年（尤其是DeFi和跨链互操作兴起后）的安全研究如何演进、协议组合时本地安全假设为何失效、以及未来Web3安全研究的关键需求。

Method: 采用四层分层架构（网络层、密码学层、共识层、应用层）结合跨域信任边界的分类方法，对区块链攻击和防御进行系统性分类和综述。

Result: 识别出区块链安全威胁从单一基础设施破坏向可编程经济滥用和跨域漏洞利用的转变趋势；揭示了协议组合时本地安全假设失效的关键问题。

Conclusion: 区块链安全研究需关注跨域信任边界下的协议组合安全性，Web3安全研究应重点解决组合性安全失效问题。

Abstract: Blockchains have evolved from simple distributed ledgers into programmable platforms that process complex application logic and carry significant financial value. All modern Web3 systems share a common goal: providing secure, decentralized, and trustworthy execution in an increasingly interconnected environment. However, this evolution has shifted the attack surface from isolated infrastructure disruptions to programmable economic abuse and cross-domain exploits. In this article, we focus on the research of blockchain attacks and defenses. In particular, we categorize the threat landscape and corresponding mitigation strategies according to both a four-tier layered architecture (network, cryptographic, consensus, and application) and cross-domain trust boundaries. We seek to answer these important questions: How has the research in blockchain security evolved over the past decade, especially with the rise of decentralized finance (DeFi) and cross-chain interoperability? How do local security assumptions fail when protocols are composed, and what are the driving needs for Web3 security research in the future?

</details>


### [112] [When Agents Remember Too Much: Memory Poisoning Attacks on Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06595)
*George Torres,Sharad Shrestha,Satyajayant Misra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对基于大语言模型的个人AI代理的新型攻击向量GhostWriter，通过毒化其长期记忆系统实现攻击，并设计了防御机制AM-Sentry。GhostWriter的注入率约98%，激活率约60%，而AM-Sentry能显著降低攻击成功率同时保持代理效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI代理融合了对话与行动规划两大领域，处理敏感信息并与不可信信息来源交互，现有长期记忆系统缺乏安全治理，导致此前未被发现的严重安全漏洞。

Method: 提出GhostWriter攻击，分两阶段：注入阶段（攻击者向目标代理发送隐藏攻击载荷）和激活阶段（被毒化的记忆被检索）。同时提出AM-Sentry防御机制，包含记忆保存策略和记忆检索屏障两种缓解技术。

Result: GhostWriter实现了约98%的近通用注入率和约60%的高平均激活率；AM-Sentry显著降低了攻击成功率，同时保持了代理的实用性能。

Conclusion: 个人AI代理的记忆系统存在严重安全缺陷，亟需安全导向的记忆治理机制；AM-Sentry作为有效防御方案，证明了可以在不牺牲代理效用的前提下抵御此类攻击。

Abstract: Personal AI agents powered by large language models can reason and act using available tools to access emails, manage calendars, and push code to remote repositories, all with minimal oversight. When augmented with long-term memory, an agent can recall specific details relevant to the current task, reducing the need for large context windows. Currently, long-term memory agents tend to fall into two distinct domains: conversational and action-planning agents. Personal assistant agents sit at the convergence of these two domains and handle sensitive information while interacting with untrusted information sources, creating previously unaccounted security vulnerabilities. In this work, we introduce the novel attack vector, GhostWriter, which exploits current memory subsystems in tool-using personal agents to poison their memory store. GhostWriter operates in two phases: injection, where an adversary sends a hidden attack payload to the target agent; and activation, in which the poisoned memory is retrieved. We show that GhostWriter achieves near-universal injection rates of approximately 98% and a high average activation rate of approximately 60% against state-of-the-art agents. This attack is possible due to the lack of security-focused memory governance. In response, we propose Agentic Memory Sentry (AM-Sentry), which leverages two mitigation techniques: a memory-saving policy and a memory-retrieval screen. Our experiments show that AM-Sentry dramatically reduces GhostWriter's success rate while preserving agent utility.

</details>


### [113] [Calibration-Family Overfit: Why Trusted Sabotage Monitors Don't Transfer Across Lineages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06596)
*Lucas Pinto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现AI控制中的可信监控器存在家族特异性：监控器在同系列（同架构/训练方法）的未信任模型上检测 sabotage 的AUROC显著高于跨家族场景，跨家族AUROC下降0.172（95% CI [0.158, 0.185]），且该交互效应并非由 sabotage 明显程度或监控器能力差异导致，而是源于不同模型家族 sabotage 在评分空间中的不同方向。在1%审计预算下，同家族监控器可捕获41%的攻击，跨家族仅捕获19%，差距21.7个百分点。作者提出四步协议以应对该差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制评估将监控器准确率报告为监控器本身属性，但作者质疑这种准确性是否部分源于监控器与未信任模型的配对关系。需验证监控器在同家族vs跨家族场景下的检测能力差异。

Method: 在代码后门数据集上，对多种开放权重模型家族（4个家族、8个家族矩阵覆盖4个前沿供应商）进行交叉家族AUROC分解，控制sabotage明显程度和监控器能力，并通过旋转评分方向验证几何解释。监控器为最大34B参数的transcript-reading模型。

Result: 跨家族AUROC下降0.172（95% CI [0.158, 0.185]），效应在多种控制条件下稳健。同家族监控器在1%审计预算下捕获41%攻击，跨家族仅19%，差距21.7个百分点。旋转评分方向可恢复大部分检测能力。

Conclusion: 控制评估应报告跨家族转移矩阵，单配对准确性高估了监控器对未经校准家族的安全性。提出四步协议量化并应对该差距。

Abstract: Trusted monitoring is a central defense in AI control: a cheaper trusted model scores an untrusted model's actions for sabotage, and the most suspicious are audited or deferred. Such monitors are evaluated against one or two untrusted models, and the accuracy is reported as a property of the monitor. We ask whether it is partly a property of the pairing. We make the untrusted policy family the controlled axis: we fit a monitor on family A's transcripts, apply it to family B, and decompose the cross-family AUROC into how obvious each family's sabotage is, how capable each monitor is, and the residual own-family advantage after both are removed: the interaction. On code-backdoor transcripts the interaction is positive and survives the dominant confounds: +0.172 (95% CI [+0.158, +0.185]) on four open-weight families on a strict leak-free basis, holding across an eight-family matrix spanning four frontier vendors, blatancy-matched controls, a trained transcript-reading monitor up to 34B parameters, and a second code benchmark. The gap is geometric: each lineage's sabotage occupies a different scoring direction, and rotating the monitor's scoring direction toward the target lineage recovers most of the lost detection while a matched random rotation recovers nothing. At a 1% audit budget under defer-to-trusted, an off-lineage monitor catches 19% of attack attempts where an in-lineage monitor catches 41% at the same audit cost, a +21.7-point gap. Control evaluations should report cross-family transfer matrices: a single-pairing accuracy overstates the safety a monitor delivers against a lineage it was not calibrated on. We give a four-step protocol that acts on the gap, with each step a measured result.

</details>


### [114] [Security and Privacy in Agentic AI: Grand Challenges and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06608)
*Adam Jenkins,Agnieszka Kitkowska,Caterina Maidhof,Diego Paracuellos,Francesco Sovrano,Gonzalo Gabriel Mendez,Guillermo Suarez-Tangil,Hana Kopecka,Isabel Wagner,Isabel Barbera,Javier Carnerero-Cano,Jide Edu,Jose Luis Martin-Navarro,Jose Such,Josep Domingo-Ferrer,Juan Carlos Carrillo,Kopo Marvin Ramokapane,Mark Cote,Pablo Vellosillo,Ramon Ruiz-Dolz,Rongjun Ma,Ruba Abu-Salma,Sameer Patil,William Seymour,Xiao Zhan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于30位国际专家参与的远见扫描活动，总结了代理型AI在安全与隐私方面的关键挑战和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理能力的不断增强，其带来的安全与隐私风险日益突出，亟需系统性识别和应对这些新兴风险。

Method: 采用远见扫描方法，召集来自学术界、工业界和政府机构的30位顶尖国际专家，通过专题讨论和协作练习，对代理型AI的新兴风险进行系统梳理。

Result: 识别出代理型AI在安全与隐私方面的关键挑战，并指出了未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 代理型AI的安全与隐私问题需要跨学科、跨部门的协作，本文为后续研究提供了重要方向性指导。

Abstract: We present key challenges and future research directions in the security and privacy of agentic AI, based on a horizon-scanning exercise that brought together thirty leading international experts from academia, industry, and government to engage in focused discussions and collaborative exercises on the emerging risks associated with the growing agency of AI.

</details>


### [115] [PRoVeFL: Private Robust and Verifiable Aggregation in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06612)
*Harsh Kasyap,Anil Kumar Pradhan,Ugur Ilker Atmaca,Graham Cormode,Carsten Maple*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PRoVeFL，一种隐私保护、拜占庭鲁棒且可验证聚合的联邦学习框架，通过多服务器多密钥全同态加密实现，支持多种鲁棒聚合算法，相比Prio和ELSA分别实现100倍和10倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习依赖中心化聚合服务器并假设客户端为诚实但好奇的，因此容易遭受服务端推理攻击和客户端投毒攻击。现有安全和拜占庭鲁棒协议在隐私、完整性和可验证性之间存在根本性权衡，且因大量使用密码学原语导致计算和通信开销巨大。

Method: PRoVeFL采用多服务器架构，利用多密钥全同态加密。每个客户端加密其本地模型更新并将加密份额分发至所有服务器。设计了混合计算模型，在严格隐私约束下将密文操作卸载到明文域，以高效评估复杂的统计聚合规则。与多种拜占庭鲁棒聚合算法兼容，并通过可验证性机制增强，仅需信任至少一个诚实服务器。

Result: 在不同设置下评估了PRoVeFL的可扩展性。相比Prio和ELSA，PRoVeFL在具有相当安全保证的条件下，分别实现了高达100倍和10倍的运行时间改进。

Conclusion: PRoVeFL成功缓解了联邦学习中隐私、鲁棒性和可验证性之间的权衡，通过模块化设计兼容多种聚合算法，并在性能上显著优于现有基于分布式信任的方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables multiple clients to collaboratively train machine learning models while retaining data locality, thereby enhancing user privacy. However, traditional FL frameworks rely on a centralized aggregation server and assume honest-but-curious clients, making them susceptible to both server-side inference and client-side poisoning attacks. Although recent work has explored secure and Byzantine-resilient FL protocols, they face a fundamental trade-off among privacy, integrity, and verifiability, and incur substantial computational and communication overhead due to the heavy use of cryptographic primitives. In this work, we propose PRoVeFL-a novel, modular FL framework that is Privacy-preserving, Byzantine-Robust, and ensures Verifiable aggregation. PRoVeFL employs multiple servers leveraging multi-key fully homomorphic encryption. Each client encrypts its local model updates and distributes encrypted shares to all servers. This design enables a hybrid computation model in which ciphertext operations are carefully offloaded to the plaintext domain under strict privacy constraints to efficiently evaluate complex statistical aggregation rules. PRoVeFL is compatible with a wide range of state-of-the-art Byzantine-robust aggregation algorithms (e.g., Krum, Trimmed Mean, FLTrust, norm clipping, MESAS, and more) and further enhances them with verifiability mechanisms that require minimal trust in at least one honest server. We evaluate it across different settings and demonstrate its scalability with varying numbers of parameters and participants. PRoVeFL improves runtime over the prior works, Prio and ELSA, based on distributed trust with comparable security guarantees, up to 100x and 10x, respectively.

</details>


### [116] [The Power of Backdoor Absorption in Community Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06643)
*Issam Seddik,Sami Souihi,Mohamed Tamaazousti,Sara Tucci Piergiovanni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了去中心化训练中后门攻击的防御问题，提出了一种结合自然吸收、随机调度和惰性验证机制的防御策略，通过将攻击注入-吸收动态建模为离散时间马尔可夫链，证明任意有界对手的成功概率渐近趋于零，仅需10%的训练步数验证即可实现零效用损失的后门抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化训练范式中，模型所有者委托外部计算提供者进行训练，攻击者可以注入低频率的隐蔽触发后门攻击并逃避审计。传统检测需要完全重计算训练步骤，带来模型所有者无法承受的计算开销，因此亟需一种计算高效的防御方法。

Method: 将攻击的注入-吸收动态形式化为离散时间马尔可夫链（DTMC）；提出结合自然吸收、随机调度器和惰性验证机制的防御策略；量化在对手控制n个训练者中f个的威胁模型下，模型所有者所需的最小审计开销以概率性地约束攻击成功率。

Result: 证明任意有界对手的成功概率渐近趋于零；仅需对10%的训练步数调用验证机制即可实现显著的后门抑制，且零效用损失。

Conclusion: 该防御方法在理论上可证明可靠且计算高效，适用于安全关键型AI系统中的后门攻击防御。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks severely threaten large-scale AI models. When model owners delegate training to external compute providers within a decentralized training paradigm, adversaries can craft stealthy, low-frequency triggers to inject malicious behavior while evading standard audits. Traditionally, detecting these attacks requires a full re-computation of the training steps--a prohibitive overhead that directly contradicts the owner's resource constraints. To address this, we investigate the resilience of continuous optimization dynamics under Byzantine perturbations, where adversaries are forced to compete against a continuous influx of honest updates. Under a threat model where an adversary compromises f out of n total trainers, we quantify the minimum auditing overhead required by the model owner to probabilistically bound the attack success rate. We formalize this injection-absorption dynamic as a Discrete-Time Markov Chain (DTMC). Using this framework, we prove that the success probability of any bounded adversary asymptotically collapses to zero under a defense strategy combining natural absorption, a randomized scheduler, and lazy verification oracle. Empirical results demonstrate significant backdoor suppression with zero utility degradation even when invoking the verification oracle on merely 10% of the total training steps. This approach yields a provably sound and computationally efficient defense for safety-critical AI.

</details>


### [117] [ORAN-DEFEND: Subspace Detection and Sanitization of Backdoor DRL xApps in Open RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06647)
*Md Raihan Uddin,Fatemeh Lotfi,Tolunay Seyfi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ORAN-DEFEND是一种无需重新训练的防御框架，通过SVD投影将受污染的DRL xApp的KPI观测投影到安全子空间，在O-RAN场景下抵御四种后门攻击，在假设成立时实现100%的回报恢复和≥99.5%的防御成功率，同时揭示了线性投影防御在触发器与合法信号共置时的固有极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN将近实时控制委托给来自第三方供应商的深度强化学习xApp，引入了新的供应链攻击面：后门策略在嵌入隐蔽触发器时会发出有害控制动作，降低服务质量。现有防御方案需要重新训练，无法直接应用于冻结的、可能已受损的xApp。

Method: 提出ORAN-DEFEND包装器，不进行重新训练，通过奇异值分解从少量可信的干净轨迹估计安全子空间，将每个KPI窗口投影到该子空间上。从理论和经验上建立了恢复条件：当触发器能量集中在安全子空间的正交补中时防御成功，并通过触发器的E⊥能量分数量化这一边界。

Result: 在Colosseum COLORAN数据集上，针对TrojDRL、SleeperNets、BadRL、Q-Incept四种结构不同的DRL后门攻击（涵盖内环和外环投毒），当子空间假设成立时，实现了100%的回报恢复和≥99.5%的防御成功率。

Conclusion: 线性投影防御存在固有极限：当触发器与合法信号共置时，E⊥能量分数单调控制恢复效果，线性残差检测器降至随机水平，而非线性分类器仍保持完美可分性，揭示了此前未被表征的防御边界。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Networks (O-RAN) increasingly delegate near-real-time control to deep reinforcement learning (DRL) xApps obtained from third-party vendors, creating a new supply-chain attack surface. A backdoor policy behaves optimally until an adversary injects a covert trigger into the observed key performance indicator (KPI) telemetry, at which point it issues harmful control actions that degrade quality of service (QoS). We present ORAN-DEFEND, a retraining-free wrapper that sanitizes a frozen, potentially compromised xApp by projecting each KPI window onto a safe subspace estimated from a small number of trusted clean rollouts via singular value decomposition (SVD). We establish, both analytically and empirically, a precise recovery condition: the defense succeeds if the trigger energy concentrates in the orthogonal complement of the safe subspace, and we quantify this boundary through the trigger's $\Eperp$ energy fraction. On the Colosseum COLORAN dataset, we evaluate four structurally distinct DRL backdoor attacks, like TrojDRL, SleeperNets, BadRL, and Q-Incept, spanning inner-loop and outer-loop poisoning regimes and demonstrate $100\%$ return recovery and $\geq99.5\%$ defense success rate across all four when the subspace assumption holds. A geometry ablation reveals an intrinsic and previously uncharacterized limit of any linear projection defense: when the trigger collocates with the legitimate signal, the $\Eperp$ energy fraction governs recovery monotonically, and the linear residual detector collapses to chance even while a nonlinear classifier retains perfect separability.

</details>


### [118] [POPS: Recovering Unlearned Multi-Modality Knowledge in MLLMs with Prompt-Optimized Parameter Shaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06649)
*Zhangheng LI,Jianing Zhu,Junyuan Hong,Sungmin Eum,Shuowen Hu,Suya You,Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性策略POPS，通过提示后缀优化和参数微调，能近乎完全恢复多模态大模型中已被"机器遗忘"的隐私信息，暴露了现有遗忘算法的根本脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在训练中可能无意编码隐私敏感数据，现有机器遗忘方法虽被提出用于强制模型遗忘隐私信息，但这些遗忘方法的鲁棒性在模型被恶意用户访问时尚未被充分挖掘。

Method: 提出Prompt-Optimized Parameter Shaking (POPS)策略：首先通过提示后缀优化（prompt-suffix optimization）诱导受害MLLM生成潜在的隐私样本，然后利用这些合成输出对模型进行微调，从而揭露真实的隐私信息。

Result: 在多个MMU基准测试上的实验表明，POPS能够实现近乎完全恢复已被遗忘的敏感信息，暴露了现有MMU算法的重大缺陷。

Conclusion: 现有基于机器遗忘的多模态隐私保护方法存在根本性脆弱性，不具备足够的鲁棒性来抵御对抗性攻击，需要更可靠的隐私保护机制。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance on cross-modal tasks by jointly training on large-scale textual and visual data, where privacy-sensitive examples could be unintentionally encoded, raising concerns about privacy or copyright violation. To this end, Multi-modality Machine Unlearning (MMU) was proposed as a mitigation that can effectively force MLLMs to forget private information. However, the robustness of such unlearning methods is not fully exploited when the model is published and accessible to malicious users. In this paper, we propose a novel adversarial strategy, namely Prompt-Optimized Parameter Shaking (POPS), aiming to recover the supposedly unlearned multi-modality knowledge from the MLLMs. Our method elicits the victim MLLMs to generate potential private examples via prompt-suffix optimization, and then exploits these synthesized outputs to fine-tune the models so they disclose the true private information. The experiments on the different MMU benchmarks reveal substantial weaknesses in the existing MMU algorithms. Our POPS can even achieve a near-complete recovery of supposedly erased sensitive information on the unlearned MLLMs, exposing fundamental vulnerabilities that challenge the foundational robustness of representative MMU-based privacy protections.

</details>


### [119] [ECO/CPO-DAG: A Contradiction-Based Accountability Layer for Adversarial Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06804)
*Sebastian Cochinescu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ECO/CPO-DAG 是一个针对对抗性供应链的问责协议，通过将矛盾检测形式化为补充验证层（而非共识机制），利用签名事件声明（ECO）构建因果有序的有向无环图（DAG），并允许观察者编译矛盾证明对象（CPO）触发经济惩罚，从而在不对供应链整体一致性做假设的前提下检测可证明的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有供应链系统缺乏对矛盾声明的形式化检测机制，且传统共识或真相建立机制在对抗性供应链场景中过于重量级，需要一个轻量、可验证的问责层来补充现有验证手段。

Method: 参与者发布签名的ECO进入因果有序的DAG，边编码happened-before关系；当两个关于同一主体的声明违反领域约束时，观察者编译CPO（包含两个签名声明及被违反规则），公开验证后触发对被确定责任方的经济惩罚。约束映射到GS1 EPCIS 2.0事件语义（空间唯一性、时间单调性、数量守恒、质量单调性、监管有效性）。支持选择性披露（承诺方案）和可选的零知识矛盾证明。

Result: 独立观察者检测模型为 1-(1-p_min)^h；k方合谋下的威慑条件为 S>g(1-p)/(kp)；存储估计算约每参与者每年1 GB。单机参考实现验证了检测模型，预测覆盖带与测量95%置信区间重叠，零误报。

Conclusion: ECO/CPO-DAG 作为补充验证层能有效检测可证明的供应链矛盾，但无法检测一致性的谎言；协议边界明确，需与源验证和预言机聚合互补。完全零知识CPO、多方传播和自适应规避仍为理论分析阶段。

Abstract: We present ECO/CPO-DAG, a domain-specific accountability protocol for adversarial supply chains that formalizes contradiction detection as a supplemental validation layer rather than a consensus or truth-establishing mechanism. Participants publish signed Event Claim Objects (ECOs) into a causally ordered, append-only directed acyclic graph (DAG) whose edges encode happened-before relations. When two claims about the same subject violate a domain constraint, any observer can compile a Contradiction Proof Object (CPO), a self-verifying object binding the two signed claims and the violated rule, which, on public verification, triggers economic slashing of a determinately blamed party. We map constraints to GS1 EPCIS 2.0 event semantics (spatial uniqueness, temporal monotonicity, quantity conservation, quality monotonicity, regulatory validity), so detection targets inconsistencies that are meaningful in practice. Selective disclosure via commitment schemes and, optionally, zero-knowledge contradiction proofs lets parties withhold claim contents until a challenge forces the minimal opening. We give an analytical treatment: an independent-observer detection model $1-(1-p_{\min})^h$, a deterrence condition $S>g(1-p)/(kp)$ under $k$-party collusion, and a storage estimate of order 1 GB per participant per year under stated assumptions. The protocol's boundary is explicit: it detects provable contradictions, not consistent lies; a party that never contradicts itself is invisible to it, so the layer complements, and does not replace, source verification and oracle aggregation. A single-machine reference implementation corroborates the detection model, with the predicted coverage band overlapping the measured 95% confidence interval at every observer count, and records zero false accusations; the fully zero-knowledge CPO, multi-party propagation, and adaptive-adversary evasion remain analytical.

</details>


### [120] [When Agents Go Rogue: Activation-Based Detection of Malicious Behaviors in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06807)
*Haowen Xu,Xue Tan,Lei Ma,Zhihao Zhang,Chao Wang,Qingze Wang,Ping Chen,Jun Dai,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出AcMAS，一种基于激活空间的检测框架，通过分析LLM智能体内部推理状态来检测MAS中的隐蔽攻击，无需依赖显式交互图，并在同步和异步场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS安全防御基于两个核心假设：语义显式的恶意攻击和基于显式图建模的拓扑结构。然而实际攻击日趋语义隐蔽，且MAS执行通常是异步的，不满足图传播模型所需的时间对齐假设，亟需一种不依赖这些假设的检测方法。

Method: 提出AcMAS框架，通过分析本地智能体激活空间中的内部推理状态来检测恶意行为。无需显式交互图，对同步和异步执行具有鲁棒性，并通过激活分析引导受感染智能体的功能恢复而非简单隔离。

Result: 在同步场景下F1达0.94（比图基线+0.22），异步场景下F1达0.93（比图基线+0.55），且在不同开源LLM骨干、攻击强度和MAS规模下表现稳定。

Conclusion: AcMAS通过激活空间分析有效解决了现有MAS防御对语义显式攻击和图建模的依赖问题，在隐蔽攻击检测和智能体恢复方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: While enabling effective collaboration on complex tasks, LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) face critical security challenges due to vulnerabilities at the agent and interaction levels. Most existing MAS security defenses are built upon two core assumptions: semantically-explicit malicious attacks and explicit graph-based modeling of the MAS topology and agent-level interactions. In practice, real-world attacks are becoming more semantically stealthy, while MAS execution is typically asynchronous without the temporal alignment assumed by graph-based propagation models. To address these limitations, we propose AcMAS, an activation-based framework for malicious-behavior detection in MAS. By analyzing internal reasoning states in the activation space of local agents, AcMAS detects even stealthy attacks in a synchronization-robust fashion, without relying on explicit interaction graphs. Moreover, our activation analysis provides critical signals to guide AcMAS in restoring the functionality of compromised agents, rather than the disruptive agent isolation commonly used by the state-of-the-art methods. Comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that AcMAS significantly outperforms graph-based baselines against stealthy attacks, by +0.22 F1 in synchronous settings (0.94 vs. 0.72) and by +0.55 F1 in asynchronous settings (0.93 vs. 0.38), with generalization across diverse open-source LLM backbones, attack intensity, and MAS scale.

</details>


### [121] [Behavioral Privacy Leakage in Agentic Negotiation: Formalizing and Mitigating Inference Attacks via Randomized Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06815)
*Barkha Rani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应随机协商策略，在保证(ε,δ)-差分隐私的同时，实现几乎必然收敛和高协商效用，在3000次双边协商实验中将对手推理准确率降低43-50%，同时协商成功率和效用保持在90%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的密码学技术只能保护显式披露的约束值，无法防止对手通过观察谈判动态（如让步轨迹、时机和收敛模式）推断私有约束，即行为隐私泄露问题。

Method: 设计了一种自适应随机协商策略，联合保证(ε,δ)-差分隐私、出价序列几乎必然收敛（当对手保留价值允许时达成协议）以及高协商效用。

Result: 在3,000次合成双边谈判中，该机制将对手推理准确率降低43-50%，同时协商成功率和效用维持在90%以上。

Conclusion: 强隐私保证可以在不显著损失性能的情况下实现，证明了隐私与协商效率之间的权衡是可控的。

Abstract: Autonomous negotiation agents are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings such as insurance and procurement. While cryptographic techniques protect explicitly disclosed constraint values, they fail to address a subtler threat: behavioral privacy leakage, where an adversary infers private constraints from observable negotiation dynamics such as concession trajectories, timing, and convergence patterns. This paper investigates behavioral differential privacy in multi-round negotiation protocols. We design an adaptive stochastic negotiation policy that jointly guarantees $(\varepsilon, δ)$-differential privacy, almost-sure convergence of the offer sequence (reaching agreement when the counterparty's reservation value permits), and high negotiation utility. Evaluated on 3,000 synthetic bilateral negotiations, our mechanism reduces adversarial inference accuracy by 43-50% while maintaining a negotiation success rate and utility above 90%, demonstrating that strong privacy guarantees can be achieved without significant loss of performance.

</details>


### [122] [Auditable Machine Unlearning for Privacy-Compliant Ransomware Detection Using Multi-Shard SISA and Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06860)
*Jannatul Ferdous,Rafiqul Islam,Md Zahidul Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合深度强化学习（DDQN）与多分片SISA重训练的可审计勒索软件检测与遗忘框架，在保持高检测性能（F1=0.9925, AUC=0.9983）的同时，实现了符合GDPR/CCPA隐私法规的选择性样本移除，并通过分片级重训练将遗忘时间从全量重训练的80-330秒降至5-30秒，且成员推断攻击分数接近0.5，表明隐私泄露风险有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的勒索软件检测器虽性能优异，但无法支持选择性移除已学习样本，这与GDPR和CCPA等隐私法规要求的用户数据删除权相冲突。亟需一种既能保持高检测性能又能合规删除特定样本的可审计检测框架。

Method: 提出可审计的检测与遗忘框架：1) 使用Double Deep Q-Network (DDQN)在非对称安全成本下从行为特征中学习奖励引导的检测策略；2) 采用多分片SISA（分片隔离状态聚合）策略，通过分片级重训练实现隐私合规的选择性样本移除，支持遗忘验证和成员推断审计。

Result: 在包含2000个样本和103个特征的Windows 11平衡行为数据集上：基线DDQN检测器F1=0.9925, AUC=0.9983；单分片遗忘保持最小效用退化与低oracle分歧；中等分片数(M=5-10)提供最佳效率-性能权衡，重训练时间从全量重训练的80-330秒降至5-30秒；成员推断分数在多数配置下接近0.5，表明遗忘后隐私泄露有限。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了一个隐私合规的勒索软件检测系统能够同时实现高检测性能、可审计的删除验证和高效的样本移除，为满足隐私法规要求的网络安全系统提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Ransomware poses an escalating cybersecurity threat as attackers continuously modify behavioral patterns to evade static defenses. Although existing machine learning-based detectors often achieve strong predictive performance, they generally assume fixed training data and do not support the selective removal of previously learned samples. This limitation conflicts with privacy regulations such as the GDPR and CCPA, which require the removal of sensitive user data upon request. To address this challenge, we propose an auditable ransomware detection and unlearning framework that integrates deep reinforcement learning with multi-shard SISA retraining. In the proposed system, a Double Deep Q-Network (DDQN) learns a reward-guided detection policy from behavioral features under asymmetric security costs, while multi-shard SISA enables privacy-compliant selective sample removal through shard-level retraining. The framework was evaluated using four criteria: utility preservation, oracle-based forgetting validation, membership inference auditing, and computational efficiency. On a balanced Windows 11 behavioral dataset comprising 2,000 samples and 103 features, the baseline DDQN detector achieved an F1 score of 0.9925 and an AUC of 0.9983. The experimental results show that single-shard unlearning maintains minimal utility degradation and low oracle disagreement, whereas moderate shard counts (M = 5-10) provide the best efficiency-performance trade-off, reducing retraining time to 5-30 s compared with 80-330 s for full retraining. In addition, the membership inference scores remain close to 0.5 across most configurations, indicating limited privacy leakage after unlearning. These findings demonstrate that a privacy-compliant ransomware detection framework can jointly achieve high detection performance, auditable deletion verification, and efficient sample removal.

</details>


### [123] [SA-DRL: Security-Aware Deep Reinforcement Learning for Ransomware Detection with Asymmetric Reward Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06880)
*Jannatul Ferdous,Rafiqul Islam,Md Zahidul Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种安全感知深度强化学习（SA-DRL）框架，通过在奖励信号中嵌入假阴性和假阳性成本不对称性来优先减少漏报，并引入安全最优模型选择（SOMS）准则与自适应样本排序机制。实验表明，非对称奖励函数能显著提升勒索软件检测的安全性能，DDQN+R2+γ=0.1配置达到漏报率0.0080，相比最优监督基线减少67.6%的漏报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习检测器使用对称目标函数，同等惩罚漏报（误判为正常）和误报（正常误判为威胁），但勒索软件场景中漏报会导致不可逆加密、运营中断和高恢复成本，而误报通常是可逆的，因此需要一种能体现这种成本不对称性的检测框架。

Method: 提出SA-DRL框架：1) 在强化学习奖励信号中嵌入假阴性与假阳性成本不对称性；2) 引入SOMS准则（优先最小化漏报率，再考虑F1分数和训练时间）；3) 自适应episode级样本排序机制。采用DQN、DDQN、PPO、A2C四种智能体，比较对称奖励(R1)与非对称奖励(R2)，使用4种折扣因子、5折交叉验证、3个随机种子，共480次训练。

Result: SOMS最优配置（DDQN+R2+γ=0.1）取得漏报率0.0080、F1分数0.9915、AUC 0.998，相比最优监督基线漏报减少67.6%。所有配置下R2相比R1平均漏报率降低43%。

Conclusion: 奖励函数设计对安全敏感的勒索软件检测至关重要，非对称奖励能够有效引导模型优先减少漏报，实现更符合安全运营需求的检测性能。

Abstract: Ransomware detection is a security-critical task in which false negatives and false positives have unequal operational consequences. Conventional machine learning detectors often use symmetric objectives that penalize missed ransomware detections and benign false alarms equally, although a false negative can cause irreversible encryption, operational disruption, and high recovery cost, whereas a false positive is usually reversible. This study proposes a Security-Aware Deep Reinforcement Learning (SA-DRL) framework that embeds false-negative and false-positive cost asymmetry into the reinforcement learning reward signal to prioritize missed-detection reduction. The framework also introduces a Security-Optimal Model Selection (SOMS) criterion and an adaptive episode-level sample-ordering mechanism. Four deep reinforcement learning agents, DQN, DDQN, PPO, and A2C, were evaluated using a symmetric baseline reward (R1) and a security-aware asymmetric reward (R2). Experiments used four discount factors, five-fold cross-validation, and three random seeds, resulting in 480 training runs on a balanced ransomware detection dataset. The SOMS criterion selects models by prioritizing false-negative rate, followed by F1-score and training time. Results show that asymmetric reward shaping improves security-oriented detection performance. The SOMS-selected configuration, DDQN with R2 and gamma = 0.1, achieved a false-negative rate of 0.0080, an F1-score of 0.9915, and an AUC of 0.998, reducing missed detections by 67.6% compared with the best supervised baseline. Across all configurations, R2 reduced the mean false-negative rate by 43% relative to R1. These findings show that reward-function design is important for security-sensitive ransomware detection.

</details>


### [124] [Large Language Models (LLMs) and Generative AI in Cybersecurity and Privacy: A Survey of Dual-Use Risks, AI-Generated Malware, Explainability, and Defensive Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06963)
*Kiarash Ahi,Saeed Valizadeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了LLM和生成式AI在网络安全中的双重作用——既用于自动化防御（实时威胁检测、钓鱼防御、安全代码生成、零日漏洞检测、DevSecOps、联邦学习、合成内容分析、XAI），也被用于发动高级攻击（LLM生成恶意软件从2021年的2%激增至2025年预计的50%）。基于70余篇文献和多个真实平台案例，提出了负责任部署LLM的建议，包括模型水印、对抗防御和跨行业协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM生成恶意软件占比从2021年的2%急剧上升至2025年的50%，高度自动化的威胁格局对下一代安全框架提出了迫切需求，亟需全面梳理LLM在网络安全中的有益与恶意应用。

Method: 综述了70余篇学术论文、行业报告和技术文档，综合分析了Google Play Protect、Microsoft Defender、AWS、Apple App Store、OpenAI Plugin Stores、Hugging Face Spaces、GitHub等平台的真实案例，以及SAFE框架和AI驱动异常检测等新兴举措。

Result: LLM和GenAI在网络安全中呈现双刃剑效应：一方面赋能实时威胁检测、钓鱼防御、安全代码生成、零日漏洞检测等防御能力；另一方面被用于生成恶意软件和发动自动化攻击，且增长迅猛。提出了模型水印、对抗防御、跨行业协作等负责任部署建议。

Conclusion: 为研究人员、工程师和安全领导者提供了在AI驱动的网络安全复杂挑战中构建安全、可扩展LLM系统的路线图，设定了AI与威胁防御交叉领域全面网络安全研究的新基准。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and generative AI (GenAI) systems, such as ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini, LLaMA, Copilot, Stable Diffusion by OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, Meta, Microsoft, Stability AI, respectively, are revolutionizing cybersecurity, enabling both automated defense and sophisticated attacks. These technologies power real-time threat detection, phishing defense, secure code generation, and vulnerability exploitation at unprecedented scales. Following a rapid surge where LLM-generated malware grew to account for an estimated 50% of detected threats by 2025, up from just 2% in 2021, navigating this highly automated threat landscape in 2026 demands next-generation security frameworks. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of the beneficial and malicious applications of LLMs in cybersecurity, including zero-day detection, DevSecOps, federated learning, synthetic content analysis, and explainable AI (XAI). Drawing on a review of over 70 academic papers, industry reports, and technical documents, this work synthesizes insights from real-world case studies across platforms like Google Play Protect, Microsoft Defender, Amazon Web Services (AWS), Apple App Store, OpenAI Plugin Stores, Hugging Face Spaces, and GitHub, alongside emerging initiatives like the SAFE Framework and AI-driven anomaly detection. We conclude with practical recommendations for responsible and transparent LLM deployment and trustworthy AI, including model watermarking, adversarial defense, and cross-industry collaboration, setting a new benchmark for rigorous, holistic cybersecurity research at the intersection of AI and threat defense, and offering a roadmap for secure, scalable LLM systems that serves as a critical reference for researchers, engineers, and security leaders navigating the complex challenges of AI-driven cybersecurity.

</details>


### [125] [Thinking More, Harnessing Better: State Machine Guided Harness Automatic Generation with Project Digestion and Workflow Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07007)
*Xing Zhang,Zikang Huang,Gang Yang,CongChong Wang,Lu Liu,Bin Yin,Mingyi Wang,Ziquan Zhao,Min Li,Zhenyu Chen,Bo Wu,Lingyun Ying*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SynapseFlow是一个通过数据流感知的函数聚合和分阶段可回滚生成工作流来自动生成高质量模糊测试工具的框架，在25个真实开源项目上相比现有工具将分支覆盖率提升1.71-4.26倍，并发现7个未知漏洞（5个已分配CVE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的模糊测试工具生成方法存在幻觉问题以及覆盖不充分的问题，原因在于粗粒度的函数定位和不对齐的生成工作流。

Method: SynapseFlow首先分析源代码构建结构流图并提取连贯的函数三元组；然后通过一个分解的四阶段流程（由分阶段回滚算法控制）来合成测试工具，确保正确性。

Result: 在25个真实开源项目上，SynapseFlow相比OSS-Fuzz-Gen、CKGFuzzer、PromeFuzz分别实现3.07倍、1.71倍、4.26倍的分支覆盖提升，以及1.77倍、1.51倍、1.36倍的漏洞检测率提升；发现了7个先前未报告的漏洞（5个已分配CVE）。

Conclusion: SynapseFlow通过数据流感知的函数聚合和分阶段回滚生成工作流，有效解决了LLM自动生成模糊测试工具中的幻觉和覆盖不足问题，在实际漏洞发现中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: High-quality fuzz harnesses are essential for effective gray-box fuzzing. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer promise for automating this task, existing one-turn generation methods suffer from hallucinations and inadequate coverage due to coarse-grained function targeting and misaligned generation workflows. We present SynapseFlow, an automatic harness generator that addresses these limitations through two key innovations: dataflow-aware function aggregation and a staged, rollback-enabled generation workflow decomposition. SynapseFlow first analyzes source code to construct Structural Flow Graphs and extract coherent Function Triplets. It then synthesizes harnesses via a decomposed four-stage process governed by a staged rollback algorithm to ensure correctness. We evaluated SynapseFlow on 25 real-world open-source software projects. The experimental results indicate that SynapseFlow outperforms state-of-the-art tools (OSS-Fuzz-Gen, CKGFuzzer, PromeFuzz), achieving 3.07$\times$, 1.71$\times$, and 4.26$\times$ higher branch coverage, and 1.77$\times$, 1.51$\times$, and 1.36$\times$ higher bug detection rates, respectively. Most importantly, SynapseFlow discovered 7 previously unreported bugs (5 assigned CVEs), demonstrating its practical effectiveness in real-world bug discovery.

</details>


### [126] [An Automated Framework for Generating Stealthy Cell-Embedded Hardware Trojans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07049)
*Raghul Saravanan,Sudipta Paria,Sai Manoj P D,Swarup Bhunia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用受损标准单元库自动生成单元嵌入式硬件木马（HT）的框架，通过在已映射的设计中识别稀有输入条件并应用有效载荷模板，在触发条件下破坏单元输出。实验表明该框架能在不同单元类型和设计规模下生成有效且隐蔽的木马，揭示了当前木马检测假设的漏洞，强调了零信任IC设计流程中对标准单元实现进行单元感知验证的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HT评估主要针对RTL或门级网表中的特洛伊植入场景建模，但无法捕捉攻击者通过受损标准单元库将恶意行为隐藏在单元实现内部的攻击方式，存在检测盲区。

Method: 提出一个自动化框架，分析已映射设计，识别具有稀有输入条件的候选单元实例，应用有效载荷模板，在触发条件满足时破坏选中单元的输出，从而生成单元嵌入式HT。

Result: 在开源组合电路和时序电路基准设计上的实验表明，该框架能够跨不同单元类型和设计规模生成有效且隐蔽的特洛伊实例。

Conclusion: 当前HT检测假设存在关键漏洞，零信任IC设计流程需要对标准单元实现进行单元感知验证。

Abstract: Hardware Trojans (HTs) pose significant threats across the Integrated Circuit (IC) design lifecycle because they can be inserted by untrusted entities at different stages under the zero-trust model. When triggered under rare conditions, HTs can compromise the functionality, reliability, or security of the fabricated chip. HT assessment is typically performed by modeling realistic Trojan insertion scenarios in RTL implementation or gate-level netlists. While this model is useful for evaluating detection methods, it does not capture attacks where malicious behavior is hidden inside standard-cell implementations from a compromised library supplied by an untrusted vendor. This paper presents a novel framework for automatically generating cell-embedded hardware Trojans using compromised standard-cell implementations. Our proposed framework analyzes a mapped design, identifies candidate cell instances with rare input conditions, and applies payload templates that corrupt the selected cell output only when the trigger condition is satisfied. Experiments on open-source combinational and sequential benchmark designs show that our proposed framework can generate valid and stealthy Trojan instances across different cell types and design sizes. The results highlight a critical gap in current Trojan detection assumptions and show the need for cell-aware validation of standard-cell implementations in zero-trust IC design flows.

</details>


### [127] [Deanonymizing Monero Transactions in Tor Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07062)
*Ruisheng Shi,Shihan Zhang,Yulian Ge,Lina Lan,Qingfeng Zhang,Qin Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Monero集成了Tor网络，但其交易转发机制存在漏洞：Tor节点发起的交易只通过两个Tor隐藏服务代理节点转发，攻击者可通过控制这些出站连接来捕获并去匿名化交易。论文提出ProxyMark框架，实现了节点角色识别、发起交易识别和节点位置去匿名化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Monero集成Tor网络后引入的隐私漏洞，证明现有隐私保护方案仍存在被攻击者利用的侧信道风险。

Method: 提出ProxyMark三阶段去匿名化框架：(1)节点角色识别；(2)发起交易识别；(3)节点位置去匿名化。通过占据目标节点的出站连接来捕获其发起的交易。

Result: 在真实Tor网络、Monero主网和测试网上的实验证明了ProxyMark能够成功地对通过Tor的Monero节点发起的交易进行去匿名化。

Conclusion: Monero与Tor的集成并非完全安全，其交易转发机制存在根本性漏洞，攻击者可通过有策略地占用出站连接来破坏匿名性。

Abstract: Monero is a privacy-focused cryptocurrency that deploys the Dandelion++ protocol and incorporates anonymity networks (such as Tor and I2P) to prevent malicious attackers from linking transactions with their source IPs. In this paper, we demonstrate that Monero's integration of the Tor network introduces a fundamental vulnerability: a Monero Tor node's originated transactions are exclusively forwarded to two outgoing Tor hidden service nodes (proxy nodes) prior to clearnet propagation, enabling an adversary to capture originated transactions by occupying the target node's outgoing connections. Based on this observation, we propose \textit{ProxyMark}, a three-stage deanonymization framework for the Monero Tor network, comprising node role identification, originated transaction identification, and node location deanonymization. Through experiments on the live Tor network, Monero mainnet, and testnet, we empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of \textit{ProxyMark} in successfully deanonymizing transactions originating from Monero nodes over Tor.

</details>


### [128] [Cyber Dynamics I: Finite Macrostates for Behavioral Anomaly Detection in Network Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07075)
*Abdul Rahman,Eranga Bandara,Sachin Shetty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于有限维宏观状态框架的网络异常检测方法，通过规范安全遥测基底（CSTS）对网络遥测数据进行粗粒化建模，利用窗口间宏观状态转移来区分良性工作负载漂移与恶意重组，相比传统熵方法提升了异常检测性能和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于熵的网络异常检测方法大多将熵视为标量统计量，仅作用于窄视野的可观测变量，未能将其置于更广泛的网络系统行为状态空间中，导致难以区分良性工作负载变化与真正的恶意行为。

Method: 提出有限维宏观状态框架，基于CSTS对网络遥测进行粗粒化，覆盖持久实体、类型化关系和时序状态；建模窗口间宏状态转移，定义体制结构、稳定性和异常变化；而非仅对异常状态进行评分。

Result: 在基准网络遥测数据集上，与Shannon、Rényi、Tsallis熵基线及标准异常检测器相比，所提表示方法提升了异常检测的判别能力，并支持更可解释的网络安全行为分析。

Conclusion: 宏观状态框架和窗口间转移建模能有效区分良性漂移与对抗性重组，为网络异常检测提供了更具可解释性和判别力的新范式。

Abstract: Entropy-based methods have long been used for network anomaly detection, but most existing approaches treat entropy as a scalar statistic on narrow observables rather than as part of a broader behavioral state-space for cyber systems. We propose a finite-dimensional macrostate framework for network telemetry, instantiated over the Canonical Security Telemetry Substrate (CSTS), so that coarse-graining is performed over persistent entities, typed relations, and temporal state rather than isolated event records. The resulting macrostate captures activity, distributional disorder, structural organization, temporal volatility, persistence, and deviation from benign baselines. Rather than scoring only unusual states, we model window-to-window macrostate transitions and define regime structure, stability, and anomalous change. This supports discrimination between benign workload drift and adversarial reorganization. We evaluate the framework on benchmark network telemetry datasets and compare it against Shannon-, Rényi-, and Tsallis-style entropy baselines, as well as standard anomaly detectors. The proposed representation improves anomaly discrimination and supports more interpretable behavioral analysis of cyber telemetry.

</details>


### [129] [Is Randomness Necessary for Adaptive Data Analysis?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07085)
*Edith Cohen,Haim Kaplan,Yishay Mansour,Shay Sapir,Uri Stemmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在随机预言机模型下证明，面对计算无界的分析师时，确定性机制最多只能支持k=Õ(n)个自适应查询，而随机机制可支持k≈n²个查询，表明随机性在自适应数据分析中是严格必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自适应数据分析(ADA)中随机机制已被充分研究，但随机性是否是必要的一直是悬而未决的基础问题。虽然针对计算有界的分析师已知随机性非必要，但对计算无界分析师的情况仍不清楚。

Method: 在信息论意义的随机预言机模型下，通过构造对抗性策略，证明任何确定性机制在面对计算无界的分析师时，经过k=Õ(n)次自适应查询后必然失败。

Result: 确定性机制最多支持k=Õ(n)个查询，而随机机制可支持k≈n²个查询，两者存在二次差距，证明随机性在信息论意义下是严格必要的。

Conclusion: 解决了ADA领域长期悬而未决的基础问题，明确了随机性在自适应数据分析中对抗计算无界分析师时的必要性，建立了确定性机制能力的基本下限。

Abstract: The Adaptive Data Analysis (ADA) problem formalizes the challenge of preventing false discovery and overfitting when a dataset is repeatedly reused. Formally, our input is a dataset containing $n$ i.i.d. samples from an unknown distribution $\mathcal{P}$ over a domain $\mathcal{X}$, and our goal is to answer a sequence of $k$ adaptively chosen statistical queries with respect to $\mathcal{P}$. The main question is how many queries we can support (i.e., how large $k$ can be), primarily as a function of the number of samples $n$. This question has been intensively studied and is relatively well-understood for randomized mechanisms: there are computationally efficient mechanisms that support $k \approx n^2$ queries, and no computationally efficient mechanism can answer $k \gg n^2$ queries. In this paper, we address a fundamental question: is randomness necessary for ADA?
  Despite a decade of work on ADA, this question remains open. A folklore observation dating back to the initial works on ADA is that randomness is not necessary when the analyst is computationally bounded. Yet, the necessity of randomness against computationally unbounded analysts has remained elusive. Our main contribution resolves this gap in the information-theoretic Random Oracle model. Perhaps surprisingly, we show that randomness is strictly necessary to answer a non-trivial number of adaptive queries: when the analyst is unbounded, any deterministic mechanism can be forced to fail after just $k = \tilde{O} (n)$ queries.

</details>


### [130] [Certifying Ghosts: How Cybersecurity AI Agents Break the EU Cyber Resilience Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07109)
*Víctor Mayoral-Vilches*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 欧盟《网络弹性法案》（CRA）假设漏洞发现是缓慢的、需要人类技能的工作，但网络安全AI（CAI）代理的出现彻底颠覆了这一前提。CRA基于四个核心假设（P1-P4）建立的合规框架，在面对CAI代理时部分失效：P1因漏洞数量激增而被迫调整（从要求完美转向要求可辩护的优先级排序），但P2、P3、P4因漏洞生命周期被压缩而彻底崩溃——产品在通过认证后可被未触碰代码的情况下被利用。解决之道与问题同源：防御者也必须使用同样的AI代理进行持续监控，而非静态的一次性认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示欧盟《网络弹性法案》（CRA）所依赖的前提假设，在网络安全AI（CAI）代理技术冲击下已经不再成立，从而指出该法案在2027年全面生效时可能面临的根本性挑战。

Method: 通过逐一分析CRA的四个核心假设（P1-P4）在CAI代理冲击下的表现，区分哪些假设能够调整适应（P1）和哪些假设彻底失效（P2、P3、P4），并在两个CRA范围内的机器人（人形机器人和割草机器人）上进行了防御性AI代理的实证验证。

Result: CRA的P1（漏洞发现是缓慢的、需要技能的人力工作）可以调整适应：合规重点转向可辩护的、有文档记录的优先级排序。但P2（产品上线时可被利用的漏洞是可知的）、P3（漏洞利用足够罕见以至于可以被注意到）和P4（修复速度跟上发现速度）彻底失效：产品通过检查后仍变得可被利用，且无需任何人触碰代码。防御者使用同样的AI代理进行持续监控可以维持安全基线。

Conclusion: 静态的、人工节奏的安全已经终结，必须转向由AI代理驱动的持续性安全合规框架。CRA在2027年全面生效时，其认证的产品将面对一个已经改变的世界，合规必须从一次性的认证转变为永不停止的运行过程。

Abstract: The EU Cyber Resilience Act (CRA) makes a smart bet. It does not demand that products be free of vulnerabilities, but only that manufacturers run a process: assess risk, handle flaws, ship updates. The bet pays off if four things about the world stay true: (P1) finding vulnerabilities is slow, skilled, human work; (P2) a product's exploitable flaws are knowable the day it ships; (P3) exploitation is rare enough to notice; and (P4) fixes keep pace with discovery. Cybersecurity AI (CAI) agents, AI put to work finding and exploiting flaws in other products, falsify all four. The regime answers in two opposite ways. Against the sheer volume of flaws that agents surface it bends (P1): built for scarce attention, it re-centres compliance on defensible, documented prioritisation, and holds. But agents also collapse the speed and economics of the vulnerability lifecycle, and here it breaks (P2, P3, P4): a product that passed every check becomes exploitable without anyone touching it, so its market-entry test, its reporting trigger, and its one-and-done certificate vouch for a security that has quietly expired. The fault is in the landscape, not the product, so running the process more diligently cannot repair it. We map each mechanism to the force that strains or snaps it, and find the cure and the disease cut from the same cloth: because defenders and attackers wield the same AI, the only conformity that survives is one that never stops running. We also carry the remedy from proposal to proof on two CRA-scope robots, a humanoid and a lawn mower, where an agentic defender holds a line their undefended selves cannot. On the evidence already in hand, the CRA reaches full force in December 2027 certifying products against a world that has already changed. Static, human-paced security is finished; what replaces it must be continuous and agent-operated, and that is no longer a matter of taste.

</details>


### [131] [Evaluating Endpoint Detection Robustness Against Genetic Algorithm Driven Code Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07191)
*Alvina Rwaichi Minja,Jema David Ndibwile*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShellForge是一个基于遗传算法的框架，用于生成功能等价的后渗透测试变体（远程命令执行），以系统评估AV/EDR端点检测系统的鲁棒性。实验发现传统签名和行为检测管线在自适应变体生成下存在可测量的鲁棒性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AV/EDR系统越来越多地结合签名检测和行为检测，但传统检测管线在面对自适应变体时的可靠性受到挑战。现有基准测试方法不足以系统评估端点检测的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出ShellForge框架，采用遗传算法（GA）驱动，应用句法变换、编码方案和结构排列，通过多目标适应度函数（结合AV/EDR检测反馈）引导变体演化，生成功能等价的后渗透远程命令执行变体。

Result: 在相同沙箱配置下，ShellForge与基准变换框架的对比表明，传统签名导向和行为导向的检测管线在受控变体生成下存在可测量的鲁棒性差距。

Conclusion: 提出了一个可重现的端点检测鲁棒性评估基准，强调了开发鲁棒性感知的防御监控和行为关联的必要性。

Abstract: Post-compromise test variants are widely used in controlled security evaluation and endpoint robustness benchmarking. However, modern Antivirus (AV) and Endpoint Detection and Response (EDR) systems increasingly combine signature- and behavior-based detection, challenging the reliability of conventional detection pipelines under adaptive variation. This study introduces ShellForge, a Genetic Algorithm (GA)-driven framework that evolves post-compromise variants representative of remote command execution to generate functionally equivalent variants for systematic detection evaluation. ShellForge applies syntactic transformations, encoding schemes, and structural permutations guided by a multi-objective fitness function informed by AV and EDR detection feedback. We compare ShellForge against representative baseline transformation frameworks under identical sandbox configurations. Our findings highlight measurable robustness gaps in baseline signature- and behavior-oriented detection pipelines under controlled variant generation. In addition, we propose a reproducible benchmark for endpoint detection robustness evaluation, motivating the need for robustness-aware defensive monitoring and behavioral correlation.

</details>


### [132] [Continual Learning With Participation Privacy: An Auditable Buffering-Aggregation Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07209)
*T-H. Hubert Chan,Elaine Shi,Mengshi Zhao,Mingxun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可审计的模块化方案，通过随机缓冲包装器将单编辑邻居流转换为汉明风格更新流，并结合自适应上下文下的隐私认证定理，实现了基于标准原语（如树前缀和）的轨迹级差分隐私，并建立了隐私与延迟之间的显式联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦学习和流式学习系统在持续发布中间模型时面临隐私挑战：单次插入/删除操作会引发后续所有更新的偏移，导致标准的汉明邻居持续发布分析失效。本文旨在解决这种单编辑邻居用户流下的轨迹级隐私保护问题。

Method: 提出了一个包含两个核心组件的模块化框架：(1) 随机缓冲包装器，将单编辑流转换为大小为[U,2U]的桶，从而规约为汉明风格的每桶更新流，其中U由隐私参数(ε,δ)校准；(2) 非自适应汉明邻居DP证明向自适应输入提升的认证定理，要求原语使用每轮新鲜随机性并在共同自适应上下文中具有稳定的一轮隐私轮廓。

Result: 该方案通过标准原语（如树前缀和）实现了单编辑流下的轨迹级(ε,δ)-DP，并建立了显式的隐私-延迟权衡关系（通过U参数调节）。

Conclusion: 该工作为流式学习中单编辑邻居场景下的隐私保护提供了可审计的模块化解决方案，弥合了自适应交互与非自适应分析之间的鸿沟，并揭示了隐私与延迟之间的定量关系。

Abstract: Modern federated and streaming learning systems often release intermediate models, so privacy must hold for the full trajectory under adaptive interaction.
