Motivated by participation privacy, we study single-edit neighboring user streams, where one insertion/deletion shifts all subsequent updates and defeats standard Hamming-neighbor continual-release analyses. We give an auditable modular recipe. A randomized buffering wrapper emits bins of size $[U,2U]$, reducing single-edit streams to a Hamming-style per-bin update stream with explicit backlog/delay guarantees, where $U$ is calibrated by the privacy parameters $(\varepsilon,δ)$. We then prove a certification theorem identifying when a non-adaptive Hamming-neighbor DP proof for a continual primitive lifts to adaptive inputs: the primitive must use fresh per-round randomness and have a stable one-round privacy profile under common adaptive context. Together, these ingredients yield trajectory-level $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP for single-edit streams using standard primitives (e.g., tree prefix sums), with an explicit privacy--latency link via $U$.

</details>


### [133] [Monitoring Vulnerabilities in Next-Generation Automotive Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07226)
*Dimitri Simon,Badis Hammi,Joaquin Garcia-Alfaro,Hervé Debar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对软件定义汽车（SDV）提出了一种结合公共CVE数据库与Docker化开发环境的系统性漏洞评估方案，解决了现有工具在操作系统层面分析多样化软件栈效率低下的问题，并评估了可利用性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件定义汽车集成了复杂的软硬件系统，面临严峻的安全挑战，而现有漏洞评估工具在处理操作系统漏洞及多样化软件栈分析时存在不足。

Method: 结合公共CVE数据库进行系统性漏洞发现，并在Docker化开发环境中评估漏洞的可利用性风险。

Result: 揭示了潜在威胁的广度以及实际利用过程中面临的实际约束。

Conclusion: 讨论了该工作对工业界和学术界的启示，并提出了构建更具弹性的SDV的未来方向。

Abstract: Software-defined vehicles (SDVs) are revolutionizing transportation by integrating complex, interconnected hardware, and software systems. This evolution introduces significant security challenges. We present a comprehensive security analysis for SDVs, focusing on software vulnerabilities. We note that existing vulnerability assessment tools fall short in addressing operating systems vulnerabilities, particularly when it comes to efficiently analyzing diverse software stacks in realistic environments. We present and release a vulnerability assessment solution that efficiently addresses these limitations. Our approach combines systematic vulnerability discovery, leveraging public Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) databases, within a dockerized development environment that evaluates exploitability risks. The results reveal both breadth of potential threats and the practical constraints we faced during exploitation. We discuss the implications for industry and research, and propose directions for building more resilient SDVs.

</details>


### [134] [MoLIFE: Methodology, Technologies, and Challenges for Mobile Live Intelligent Forensics Examination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07269)
*Silvia Lucia Sanna,Cristina Alcaraz,Alessandro Sanna,Giorgio Giacinto,Javier Lopez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MoLIFE的新型移动设备数字取证方法论，基于NIST SP800-101标准，结合AI、区块链、量子计算等新兴技术，用于移动设备的数据获取与分析，并通过Android设备数据获取案例验证了技术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备数据保护机制（如Android和iOS的超级用户保护）日趋严格，导致物理获取或分析特定目录会破坏证据，而绕过保护需要提权或自定义ROM安装，会改变设备状态；同时移动技术需求激增带来大量安全威胁，因此需要一种不破坏设备状态的移动设备数字取证方法。

Method: 基于NIST SP800-101标准的数字取证流程，提出MoLIFE方法论；该方法可集成AI、区块链、量子计算等新兴技术；通过mDT工具在Android设备上进行数据获取的小型案例研究来验证技术可行性。

Result: MoLIFE方法论在技术上是可行的，能够通过mDT工具在Android设备上进行数据获取；该方法可应用于网络威胁预防和数字取证事后分析；但因其依赖新兴技术，在几年内可能受限于这些技术的局限性。

Conclusion: MoLIFE是一种有前景的移动设备实时智能取证方法，能够适应未来技术发展，但其有效性取决于新兴技术的成熟度，目前的局限性有望在未来数年内被克服。

Abstract: Nowadays, mobile forensics is less explored in Digital Forensics case analysis due to the increase in data protection mechanisms implemented by tech companies (i.e., Google for Android and Apple for iOS). For example, the physical acquisition or analysis of specific directories under super-user protection would corrupt the evidence; access to such data is protected, and bypassing this protection requires either privilege escalation or custom ROM installation, leading to the modification of the device state. At the same time, the demand for mobile technologies and their respective communication systems is increasing exponentially, exposing numerous security threats and risks. For that reason, this paper presents a Mobile Live Intelligent Forensics Examination (MoLIFE), a novel Digital Forensics (DF) methodology for data acquisition and analysis of mobile devices. The proposed methodology is based on NIST SP800-101 for the DF process. MoLIFE can be integrated with new and emerging technologies by exploiting their power (e.g., AI, blockchain, quantum computing). MoLIFE can also be used to prevent cyber threats and incidents, as well as DF post-mortem analysis, offering examples of applying the MoLIFE methodology and good practices for the future. To prove the technical feasibility of the methodology, a small case study on Android devices data acquisition via the mDT will be presented. As the methodology is based on new and emerging technologies, it depends on their limitations that would be overcome in a few years.

</details>


### [135] [Safe2Hail: A Forensic-Driven Post-Trip Tracking Framework for Ride-Hailing Safety in Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07271)
*Alvina Minja,Robert Maina,Mahmud Oloyede,Jema Ndibwile*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Safe2Hail是一个法医学驱动的框架，通过临时的行程后同步机制持续记录乘客与司机之间的邻近数据，以解决网约车行程取消或提前终止后的安全保障问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲网约车应用虽提供了更安全、更实惠的出行服务，但行程取消或提前终止后仍存在安全威胁，现有安全措施（如SOS警报、实时位置共享）仅在行程期间有效，无法覆盖行程结束后的安全盲区。

Method: Safe2Hail框架结合法医学追踪与临时性行程后同步机制，在事件发生后安全记录乘客与司机之间的所有邻近数据。该框架在部分受到内罗毕和达累斯萨拉姆真实犯罪事件启发的设计基础上，进行了beta测试。

Result: Beta测试证明了Safe2Hail框架的有效性，显示其具有可行性、资源轻量级以及可扩展性。但研究团队未进行实际部署试点。

Conclusion: Safe2Hail框架能够实现行程结束后的持续安全保障，填补了现有安全措施在行程后阶段的空白，具有轻量级和可扩展的优势。

Abstract: Ride-hailing mobile apps have become an essential feature in the mobility ecosystem in Africa, offering much safer and much more affordable rides. Although user bases have increased and the number of daily trips has proliferated, reports of imminent safety threats, particularly after the cancellation of the ride or the ride is prematurely terminated, remain unresolved challenges. Current safety measures offer features such as SOS alerts, safety notifications, and live location sharing for the duration of the trip, but they are not in place when the trip is over. Safe2Hail presents a framework that is forensically driven to ensure continuous safety and certainty beyond the trip. The Safe2Hail framework combines forensic tracking with a temporary post-trip synchronization mechanism that can securely log all proximal data between a passenger and a driver after an event. The Safe2Hail framework was beta-tested and demonstrates the effectiveness of the system. Although the research team did not pilot on actual deployment, the Safe2Hail design format was in part inspired by actual crime events reported in Nairobi and Dar-es-Salaam. The findings of the study referenced Safe2Hail's feasibility, light weight nature of resources, as well as the scalability for the framework.

</details>


### [136] [The AI Resilience Gap: Bringing Artificial Intelligence Inside the Operational Resilience Perimeter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07359)
*Jonathan Shelby*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出当前以可信赖为中心的人工智能治理体系（如欧盟AI法案、ISO标准等）不足以应对AI运营韧性挑战，提出AI韧性框架（AI Resilience Framework），通过依赖映射、关键性-可替代性分层、影响容忍度扩展、回退策略和供应商集中度管理，将AI依赖纳入运营韧性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI治理体系（可信赖AI）聚焦于安全、公平、透明和模型风险，但未覆盖运营韧性——即重要业务服务在严重但合理的中断下保持连续性、AI组件的可替代性，以及对少数前沿模型供应商的依赖集中度问题。这一结构性缺口急需填补。

Method: 首先映射可信赖AI与运营韧性两种监管逻辑，识别二者之间的结构性缺口；然后提出AI韧性框架（AI Resilience Framework），包含五个核心要素：依赖映射、关键性-可替代性分层、AI特定失效模式的影响容忍度扩展、明确的回退策略、以及供应商层面的集中度管理。

Result: 构建了一个与监管体制无关的AI韧性框架，为CISO、安全架构师和董事会提供了从AI治理政策到可验证韧性的可操作路径。该框架可扩展至英国网络安全韧性监管体系的人工智能维度。

Conclusion: AI采纳创造了一种区别于可信赖AI、且未被现有框架充分覆盖的韧性责任。英国金融监管机构（金融政策委员会的系统性分析、关键第三方制度、2026年5月前沿AI与网络韧性联合声明）正在填补这一缺口。AI韧性框架可作为通用的方法论补充。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of artificial intelligence across regulated firms has produced an extensive governance response oriented around trustworthiness: the EU AI Act, ISO IEC 42001, the NIST AI Risk Management Framework, and the United Kingdom's principles-based approach all address safety, fairness, transparency, and model risk. That response is necessary but incomplete. It does not, on its own, address operational resilience: the continuity of important business services under severe but plausible disruption, the substitutability of AI components, and the concentration of dependency on the small number of firms that supply frontier models. This paper argues that AI adoption creates a resilience obligation that is distinct from, and inadequately covered by, the trustworthy AI stack, and that United Kingdom financial authorities are already closing this gap through the Financial Policy Committee's systemic analysis, the Critical Third Parties regime, and the May 2026 joint statement on frontier AI and cyber resilience. We map the two regulatory logics, identify the structural gap between them, and propose the AI Resilience Framework: a regime-agnostic method for bringing AI dependencies inside the operational resilience perimeter through dependency mapping, a criticality-substitutability tiering, the extension of impact tolerances to AI-specific failure modes, an explicit fallback doctrine, and provider level concentration management. The framework gives chief information security officers, security architects, and boards an actionable route from AI governance policy to demonstrable resilience. This work extends a companion analysis of the United Kingdom cyber resilience regulatory stack into the artificial intelligence dimension.

</details>


### [137] [zk-ScalHard: Scalable and Hardware-Rooted Privacy-Preserving Authentication for Secure OTA Updates in Zonal SDVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07371)
*Shrikant Tangade,Bansi Pambhar,Valeria Loscri,Mauro Conti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出zk-ScalHard协议，一种基于硬件（PUF）和零知识证明（ZKP）的去中心化认证方案，用于下一代分区架构软件定义汽车（ZoA SDVs），相比Uptane实现99.2%带宽降低和99.9%攻击面缩减，且满足GDPR合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PKI认证在ZoA架构下因ECU数量激增导致带宽瓶颈、隐私泄露和集中化风险，无法满足下一代分区SDVs对去中心化、可扩展和隐私保护认证的需求。

Method: 提出zk-ScalHard协议，包含：(1) 基于硅物理不可克隆函数（PUF）的硬件根信任；(2) 两种新型零知识证明电路——ZIDI（区域身份与完整性）和HPCA（高性能计算聚合）；(3) 利用多方计算（MPC）和递归聚合实现去中心化与可扩展性；(4) 分层去中心化信任提升模型。

Result: 与Uptane对比：通信复杂度从O(n)降至O(1)；认证带宽降低99.2%；时间攻击面降低99.9%；实现100%车辆级数据主权。

Conclusion: zk-ScalHard为未来分区架构SDVs提供了一种可扩展、安全且符合GDPR的认证架构，解决了PKI方案在带宽、隐私和集中化方面的根本性缺陷。

Abstract: The automotive industry is transitioning to Zonal-oriented Architectures (ZoA) for Software-Defined Vehicles (SDVs), enabling frequent over-the-air (OTA) updates for 100+ Electronic Control Units (ECUs). While OTA updates improve efficiency, they introduce safety-critical security risks. Current standards like Uptane and AUTOSAR Adaptive rely on Public-Key Infrastructure (PKI). However, PKI-based authentication creates bandwidth bottlenecks in in-vehicle and vehicle-to-cloud (V2I) communication as ECU density increases. It also risks exposing sensitive vehicle configurations and passenger privacy due to centralized architectures. Next-generation Zonal SDVs require decentralized, scalable authentication with data privacy. To address this, we propose zk-ScalHard, a hardware-rooted, privacy-preserving authentication protocol. We introduce a decentralized, hierarchical trust-promotion model utilizing Silicon Physical Unclonable Functions (PUFs) and two novel Zero-Knowledge Proof (ZKP) circuits: (1) Zonal Identity and Integrity (ZIDI) and (2) High-Performance Computing Aggregation (HPCA). These circuits employ multi-party computation (MPC) and recursive aggregation to achieve decentralization and scalability. The integration of ZKPs and PUFs ensures 100% vehicle-level data sovereignty. Benchmarked against Uptane, zk-ScalHard achieves constant O(1) communication and verification complexity, improving upon the linear O(n) complexity of current systems. Evaluation shows a 99.2% reduction in authentication bandwidth and a 99.9% reduction in the temporal attack surface. Our results demonstrate that zk-ScalHard provides a scalable, secure, and GDPR-compliant architecture for future Zonal SDVs.

</details>


### [138] [How Reliable Is the Multi-Input Heuristic for Bitcoin Address Clustering in Law Enforcement Contexts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07414)
*Leopold Müller,Jana Elsner,Thomas Niedermayer,Bernhard Haslhofer,Thomas Goger,Niklas Kühl,Christian Rückert*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文使用来自欧洲加密货币服务商的真实实体映射数据，系统评估了多输入启发式聚类方法（MIH）在地址聚类中的表现，发现其在完整聚类和实体层面存在显著偏差，警示在刑事取证中需谨慎使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIH是区块链取证中最广泛使用的地址聚类启发式方法，但长期以来缺乏基于可靠真实数据的系统评估。现有评价多依赖假设或小规模数据，无法反映实际执法场景中的可靠性。

Method: 构建可复用的评估框架，包含9项已建立的指标；利用欧洲加密资产服务提供商在法律要求下报告的真实地址-实体映射数据作为ground truth；分别从报告地址层面、完整聚类层面和实体层面进行多维度评估。

Result: 在仅限报告地址的评估中，MIH表现较强（召回率0.71，无合并错误）；但在完整聚类层面精度和召回率分别降至0.36和0.44；实体层面部分服务几乎完全失败。

Conclusion: MIH的可靠性高度依赖于评估指标选择和具体实体，在刑事取证、资产扣押和法庭证据等场景中，检察官和法官必须充分考虑这些局限性。

Abstract: Address clustering is an important technique in blockchain forensics, widely employed by law enforcement to trace illicit crypto asset flows. The multi-input heuristic (MIH), which clusters addresses potentially associated with the same entity, is the most widely used. Yet, despite its broad adoption, the MIH has rarely been evaluated against reliable ground truth data. We implement a reusable evaluation framework covering nine established metrics and apply it to ground truth address-to-entity mappings obtained directly from European crypto asset service providers under legally mandated reporting obligations. When evaluation is restricted to reported addresses, the MIH appears strong at dataset level: we observe no mergers between reported services and recover same-service address pairs with recall $0.71$. However, this result is driven by one large service and ignores unlabeled addresses absorbed into full clusters. Metrics that assess the full clusters show substantially lower precision and recall ($0.36$ and $0.44$), meaning that services are often only partially recovered or embedded in larger clusters. Entity-level results further reveal near-complete failures for some services. When MIH-based clusters are used to support criminal suspicion, preliminary seizure of crypto assets to secure later forfeiture/ confiscation, or as evidence in trial proceedings, prosecutors and judges must account for the heuristic's metric-dependent and entity-dependent reliability.

</details>


### [139] [Beware of Agentic Botnets: Scalable Untargeted Promptware Attacks via Universal and Transferable Adversarial HalluSquatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07433)
*Aya Spira,Stav Cohen,Elad Feldman,Ron Bitton,Avishai Wool,Ben Nassi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"对抗性幻觉抢注"的攻击技术，利用LLM在资源标识符上的幻觉倾向，通过预注册流行资源名称来托管对抗性提示，从而在不依赖直接通道的情况下大规模放大无目标提示注入攻击，甚至可建立僵尸网络。实验显示幻觉生成率高达85%-100%，并在多个生产级LLM应用中实现了远程工具执行和远程代码执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已表明攻击者可通过直接通道对LLM应用实施提示注入攻击，但许多应用并不提供可被利用的直接通道。因此，研究问题是在缺乏直接通道的实际威胁模型下，攻击者能否大规模利用LLM应用。

Method: 提出对抗性幻觉抢注技术：攻击者识别流行资源（如仓库、技能等），计算LLM在这些资源名称上的幻觉分布，预注册被LLM幻觉生成的资源名称，并在其中托管对抗性提示。利用幻觉在基础LLM及应用的各层之间的可预测性和可迁移性来放大攻击范围。

Result: 仓库克隆场景下幻觉生成率达85%，技能安装场景下达100%；幻觉在基础模型和不同提示间具有可迁移性；在生产级LLM应用（集成终端的工具集）中成功实现远程工具执行和远程代码执行。

Conclusion: LLM的幻觉特性可被利用来在缺乏直接通道的情况下发起大规模无目标提示注入攻击，并能建立僵尸网络，揭示了新型安全威胁向量。

Abstract: The growing adoption of agentic LLM applications has introduced a new threat previously named as promptware. While prior work has established that adversaries can exploit direct channels to LLM applications to apply promptware under weak threat models, many applications do not provide any direct channels that could be exploited for prompt injection beyond the Internet. This raises a question: can attackers exploit LLM applications at scale without any direct channels in practical threat models? In this work, we show that the inherent tendency of LLMs to hallucinate resource identifiers can be exploited to amplify untargeted promptware attacks that pull adversarial prompts at scale and could be exploited to establish a botnet. We introduce adversarial hallucination squatting, a technique in which attackers identify trending resources (e.g., popular repositories, popular skills, etc.), compute the LLM distribution of hallucinations on the trending resource names, and preemptively register them to host adversarial prompts. By leveraging the predictability and transferability of hallucinations across foundational LLMs and to application layers, adversaries can significantly amplify the reach of untargeted promptware under weak threat models and establish a botnet by exploiting LLM applications to install a bot on the device that pulled the compromised hallucinated resource from the Inter. We empirically demonstrate that hallucinated resource generation occurs at high rates, up to 85% in repository cloning scenarios and up to 100% in skill installation, and that these hallucinations transfer between foundational models and different prompts. We demonstrate the practicality of adversarial hallucination squatting against various production LLM applications with integrated terminals in their set of tools, achieving remote tool execution and remote code execution.

</details>


### [140] [Mitigating Taint-Style Vulnerabilities in MCP Servers via Security-Aware Tool Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07461)
*Yang Shi,Jiaheng Fu,Yihe Huang,Ruixiang Wu,Chengyao Sun,Kaifeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了MCP服务器中的污点类漏洞，并提出SPELLSMITH——一种通过协议描述属性和LLM自反思机制主动缓解漏洞的统一策略，实验证明其有效性优于传统代码级修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议扩展了LLM作为自主代理的攻击面，但现有研究对MCP服务器漏洞的元数据特征、易受攻击的代码模式及社区响应情况缺乏系统分析，且污点类漏洞占比高、修复成本大、社区响应慢，亟需一种通用的主动缓解方案。

Method: SPELLSMITH通过分析MCP服务器暴露的高风险能力，结合工具描述和参数语义构建工具级风险画像；利用协议描述属性嵌入行为引导（描述增强模块），并借助LLM自反思能力（自反思模块）迭代评估和优化输出，从而在LLM内部决策层面实现主动防御。

Result: 实验表明SPELLSMITH有效缓解了MCP服务器中的污点类漏洞利用，在实用性和泛化能力上优于传统代码级缓解措施。

Conclusion: SPELLSMITH提供了一种不依赖上下文特定代码修复的统一主动缓解策略，能跨多种漏洞泛化，对MCP生态系统的安全性提升具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents that interact with external tools and services via the Model Context Protocol (MCP), a standardized interface for dynamic tool invocation. While MCP simplifies integration, it also expands the attack surface and enables generic exploits across multiple servers. Despite prior work on malicious MCP servers, the vulnerability landscape of MCP servers remains underexplored. In this work, we systematically analyze MCP server vulnerabilities, focusing on metadata characteristics, vulnerable code patterns, and community responses. Our study reveals that taint-style vulnerabilities constitute a substantial fraction of MCP server vulnerabilities, require significant code modifications to remediate, and are met with slow community responses. Motivated by these findings, we propose SPELLSMITH, presenting a novel textbased avenue for shielding taint-style vulnerabilities in MCP servers. In particular, SPELLSMITH analyzes the high-risk capabilities exposed by an MCP server and combines them with tool descriptions and parameter semantics to identify potential taint-style vulnerability risks, thereby constructing a tool-level risk profile. Then, SPELLSMITH leverages the Description property of the protocol to embed behavioral guidance (Description Enhancement Module) and exploits LLMs' self-reflection capabilities (Self-Reflection Module) to iteratively evaluate and refine outputs. By strengthening LLM internal decision-making, SPELLSMITH provides an active and unified mitigation strategy that generalizes across multiple vulnerabilities, reducing reliance on context-specific code-level fixes. Our experiments demonstrate that SPELLSMITH effectively mitigates taint-style vulnerability exploitation in MCP servers, highlighting its practical applicability and advantages over traditional code-level mitigations.

</details>


### [141] [Beyond Attack-Success Rate: Action-Graded Severity Scale for Tool-Using AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07474)
*Harry Owiredu-Ashley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种行为分级危害评估框架（L0-L6），用以替代传统红队测试中二元的攻击成功/失败指标，通过评估智能体工具调用轨迹的可逆性、范围跨越和权限扩展三个维度，更精细地衡量攻击的实际危害程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统红队测试仅以二元攻击成功率报告智能体是否被攻陷，这丢失了防御者最需要的信息——即攻击行为实际造成的危害程度。作者认为需要一种更细粒度的评估方法。

Method: 提出七级序数分级量表（L0-L6），依据工具调用轨迹的可逆性、是否跨越作用域触及第三方、是否扩展权限三个维度评分。通过两种方式计算：确定性预言机（读取轨迹和攻击目标）和三个前沿语言模型组成的评审小组（读取无标签的轨迹）。在AgentDojo工作区套件上对4个受害模型和2种防御进行实验。

Result: 危害分级揭示了二元指标隐藏的三种情况，包括一种防御报告零攻击成功率仍通过未过滤工具产生外部可见的跨作用域泄漏。评审小组与预言机具有高度序数一致性（Krippendorff's alpha=0.91），但存在系统性盲点，特别是未能识别升级链。

Conclusion: 本文贡献是一个可复用、基于轨迹的危害评估工具，应用于现有红队日志中的实际行为记录，区别于前人提供危害分类学、有害任务完成测试、执行级安全基准或危害感知模拟的工作。

Abstract: Agentic red-teaming benchmarks report whether an injected agent was compromised as a single bit: the attack succeeded, or it did not. We argue that this binary attack-success rate discards the information a defender most needs, namely how harmful the resulting action was. We introduce an action-graded harm rubric that scores an agent's tool-call trajectory on a seven-level ordinal scale (L0 to L6) according to whether the executed action was reversible, whether it crossed scope to reach another party, and whether it expanded privilege. We compute the scale two ways: a deterministic oracle that reads the trajectory and the attacker's stated goal, and a panel of three frontier language-model judges that read a tag-free account of the same trajectory. Across four victim models and two defenses on the AgentDojo workspace suite, severity grading exposes three cases the binary metric hides, including a defense that reports a zero attack-success rate while still permitting an externally visible cross-scope leak through an unfiltered tool. The judge panel reproduces the oracle with high ordinal agreement (Krippendorff's alpha = 0.91) but shares systematic blind spots that we characterize, most notably a failure to recognize escalation chains. Unlike prior work that provides harm taxonomies, harmful-task completion tests, execution-level safety benchmarks, or severity-aware simulation, our contribution is a reusable, trace-grounded severity instrument applied to the actual actions recorded in existing red-team logs. All code, prompts, and per-episode logs are released.

</details>


### [142] [NARAD: Non-colluding Aggregator-oblivious Record-And-Decrypt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07596)
*Akshit Vakati Venkata,Rajat Dugar,Ayush Adarsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Paillier同态加密的无信任密钥分发者电子投票架构，利用Solana区块链实现不可篡改记录，通过分离收集器与聚合器角色保护隐私，支持位打包提升效率，并在可信执行环境中防篡改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子投票需要在保证选票隐私的同时实现公开可验证的计票结果，但现有方案通常依赖可信密钥分发者，存在单点信任风险。

Method: 每位选民在浏览器中用自生成密钥对选票进行Paillier同态加密；收集器将辅助值聚合成单一群元素，聚合器利用该元素消去随机掩码恢复投票总和；Solana区块链记录密文并强制执行选举生命周期；使用原生C库libtommath执行大数运算；位打包方案将多候选人选票压缩至单个密文；收集器和聚合器运行在经认证的安全飞地中。

Result: 在b=25、255位模数下支持10位候选人及每位候选人最多约3350万张选票，总计约3.35亿张选票；5万张选票的计票时间低于1秒。

Conclusion: 该方案在无需可信密钥分发者的前提下同时实现了选票隐私与公开可验证性，通过位打包显著降低计算与存储开销，安全飞地进一步防止角色间共谋去匿名化，整体架构具有可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Electronic voting must keep individual ballots private while letting anyone verify the final tally. This paper presents an architecture that meets both goals without a trusted key dealer: each voter encrypts a ballot in the browser with a self-generated secret key under the Paillier additive homomorphic cryptosystem, and no party ever holds every key. Two server roles divide the tally. A collector combines the voters' per-ballot auxiliary values into a single group element; an aggregator uses that element to cancel the voters' random masks inside the homomorphic product and recover the exact vote sum, learning the result but no individual ballot. The Solana blockchain records every ciphertext immutably and enforces the election lifecycle, while a native C library (libtommath) performs the heavy modular arithmetic. We state six assumptions under which the protocol is correct and prove product homomorphism, mask cancellation, and sum recovery; privacy rests on the Decisional Composite Residuosity (DCR) assumption for the additive layer together with a Diffie-Hellman-style assumption on the masking base. A bit-packing scheme places an entire multi-candidate ballot in one ciphertext, cutting client work, on-chain transactions, storage, and tally cost by a factor of k (the candidate count); the slot width b is free, with only k*b bounded by log_2(N). With b = 25 and a 255-bit modulus the scheme supports ten candidates and up to 2^25 - 1 = 33,554,431 votes per candidate, about 335 million ballots, and tallies 50,000 ballots in under one second. Finally, running the collector and aggregator inside attested secure enclaves makes the tally tamper-resistant and prevents cross-role collusion to deanonymize voters. The proof-of-concept implementation is open-source; a worked numerical example in the appendix reproduces the full pipeline.

</details>


### [143] [Embedded Blockchain Infrastructure Management (eBIM): A RISC-V-Empowered Hardware--Software Co-Design Framework Towards Trustworthy Blockchain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07625)
*Qinglin Yang,Yuan Liu,Yaoyao Zhang,Boya Wang,Zongjian You,Chunming Rong,Zhihong Tian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出eBIM（嵌入式区块链基础设施管理）概念，即基于RISC-V开放架构的软硬件协同区块链基础设施管理范式，旨在克服区块链即服务（BaaS）模式中信任、灵活性和数据主权受限的问题，并为区块链密码加速、可信执行环境、零知识虚拟机及智能合约执行引擎等方向提供基础参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链从数字资产账本向通用可信基础设施演进，但现有的区块链即服务（BaaS）模型依赖第三方服务商提供基础设施，导致信任、灵活性和数据主权的系统性丧失。为克服这一结构性缺陷，需要一种开放、低层级、可编译、可验证且可扩展的计算基础。

Method: 受eSIM启发，基于RISC-V开放、模块化、可扩展的指令集架构，定义了一种软硬件协同的区块链基础设施管理范式——eBIM（嵌入式区块链基础设施管理）。通过综述方式回答三大研究问题：eBIM是什么、如何工作、能做什么。

Result: 构建了eBIM概念框架，涵盖密码加速、可信执行环境、零知识虚拟机及智能合约执行引擎等关键研究领域，为研究人员、硬件架构师和协议设计者提供了基础性参考。

Conclusion: eBIM为克服BaaS模型的信任瓶颈提供了可行路径；未来研究方向包括进一步深化软硬件协同设计与RISC-V生态的整合。

Abstract: Blockchain systems are undergoing a fundamental transition from decentralized ledgers for digital assets to general-purpose trust infrastructures for verifiable computation, decentralized physical resources, and automated infrastructure management. Meanwhile, the limitations of the Blockchain as a Service (BaaS) model stem from a common structural problem: outsourcing control of infrastructure to third-party service providers inevitably involves a systemic surrender of trust, flexibility, and data sovereignty. RISC-V, with its open, modular, and extensible design, provides a general-purpose computing foundation for public blockchains that is open, low-level, compileable, verifiable, and scalable. Inspired by the development and characteristics of eSIM, the embedded Blockchain infrastructure management (eBIM) is defined as a software-hardware collaborative paradigm for blockchain infrastructure management with RISC-V. This study aims to provide a comprehensive survey on eBIM supporting research and technologies, to answer the following research questions (RQs): RQ1 What is eBIM? RQ2 How does eBIM work? RQ3 What can eBIM do? By introducing the concept of eBIM, this paper establishes a foundational reference for researchers, hardware architects, and protocol designers in this rapidly evolving landscape, including cryptographic acceleration, trusted execution environments, zero-knowledge virtual machines, and smart contract execution engines. The prospects of the proposed e-BIM and its future research directions are indicated in this paper.

</details>


### [144] [Unlearning to Protect: A Distilled Reinforcement Learning Framework with Privacy-Preserving Feature Unlearning and XAI for IoT Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07635)
*Md. Nahid Hasan,Golam Rabiul Alam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DiRLU，一种轻量级、基于强化学习的僵尸网络检测框架，结合知识蒸馏、A2C算法和选择性遗忘机制，在BoT-IoT数据集上达到99.60%准确率和99.80% F1分数，仅需2370 FLOPS，比现有最优模型高效3.87倍，同时满足GDPR隐私合规要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备缺乏内置的僵尸网络流量过滤能力，现有AI方案存在两个局限：部署时计算负担过重，且缺乏在不重新训练的情况下遗忘敏感或过时特征的能力。

Method: 采用知识蒸馏将教师模型知识迁移至轻量级学生模型，两者均使用A2C强化学习算法训练；通过事后遗忘机制修改权重以移除特定特征，同时保留可逆恢复能力；使用LIME实现可解释AI。

Result: 学生模型达到99.60%准确率和99.80% F1分数；仅需2370 FLOPS，比SOTA模型高效3.87倍；使用BoT-IoT数据集25%数据（优于基准模型的5%）；遗忘特征后恢复的性能损失可忽略。

Conclusion: DiRLU在保持高检测性能的同时实现了轻量化和隐私合规（符合GDPR被遗忘权），为物联网安全提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Botnets pose a significant cybersecurity threat, enabling attacks such as DDoS, data theft, and service disruptions on IoT devices. These devices often lack built-in botnet traffic filtering, leaving them highly exposed. Existing AI-based solutions improve detection capabilities but have limitations: (i) they are too heavy for IoT deployment, and (ii) they lack unlearning capabilities to forget sensitive or outdated features without retraining. To address these challenges, we propose DiRLU, a lightweight, reinforcement learning driven framework, while ensuring privacy by selectively unlearning sensitive or outdated features without requiring retraining. The framework leverages knowledge distillation to transfer knowledge from a teacher model into a lightweight student model, with both models trained using A2C. A post-hoc unlearning mechanism modifies weights to remove targeted features, while restored features show negligible performance loss, confirming reversibility. Unlike many benchmark models that used only 5% of the BoT-IoT dataset, this research leverages 25%, allowing us to develop a strong teacher model. Both the teacher and student models were trained using the A2C reinforcement learning algorithm, achieving impressive results, with the student model achieving 99.60% accuracy and a 99.80% F1 score. To enhance transparency, we integrated Explainable AI (XAI), particularly LIME, which helps interpret the model's decisions and identify the key features influencing its predictions. Moreover, DiRLU requires only 2,370 FLOPS, approximately 3.87x more efficient than the state-of-the-art model, highlighting its efficiency for edge deployment. DiRLU combines efficiency with privacy, aligning with GDPR standards (right to be forgotten) to provide practical and scalable IoT security solution.

</details>


### [145] [Modeling Failure Dynamics in Time-Constrained Authentication Systems: Evidence of a Success Cliff in USSD Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07650)
*Aklile Seyoum Mamo,Amanuel Kebede,Anny Christelle Irakoze,Jema Ndibwile*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究USSD等时间受限交互系统中，认证复杂度与用户响应时间、网络往返时间如何共同影响会话成功率，提出并定义了“成功悬崖”（Success Cliff）这一非线性失败现象，并通过仿真框架量化了安全性与可用性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间受限的交互系统（如基于USSD的金融服务）在严格的会话限制和顺序用户交互下运行，更强的认证机制虽然提升了安全性，但增加了交互复杂性和时间负担，可能导致交易完成率下降。作者希望量化这种安全与可用性之间的权衡。

Method: 建立故障动态模型，提出并实现基于仿真的框架，研究认证复杂度、用户响应时间和网络往返时间对会话成功率的影响，形式化定义了“成功悬崖”非线性失败现象，并通过控制实验进行验证。

Result: 发现了会话成功率在超过临界复杂度阈值后急剧下降的非线性现象（成功悬崖），并量化了安全性与可用性之间的权衡，识别了安全认证工作流变得操作上不可靠的条件。

Conclusion: 在时间受限的交互系统中，认证复杂度存在一个临界阈值，超过该阈值后会话成功率会急剧下降，安全认证工作流在特定条件下会变得操作上不可靠，需要在安全性与可用性之间谨慎权衡。

Abstract: Time-constrained interactive systems such as USSD (Unstructured Supplementary Service Data)-based financial services operate under strict session limits and sequential user interaction. While stronger authentication mechanisms improve security, they also increase interaction complexity and time burden, potentially reducing transaction completion. In this work, we model the failure dynamics of such systems and investigate how authentication complexity interacts with user response time and network round-trip time to influence session success rate. We propose and implement a simulation-based framework to investigate these failure dynamics and formally define a non-linear failure phenomenon, termed the \textit{Success Cliff}, where session success rates sharply decline beyond a critical complexity threshold. Through controlled experiments, we quantify the trade-off between security and usability and identify conditions under which secure authentication workflows become operationally unreliable.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Statistical inverse learning and $\ell^1$-regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07468)
*Abhishake Rastogi,Tatiana A. Bubba,Tapio Helin,Luca Ratti*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究从有限、含噪、间接观测中恢复稀疏函数的统计逆学习问题。提出基于ℓ1正则化的经验风险最小化器，建立了几乎必然一致性和非渐近高概率收敛率，并证明了匹配的极小化最优下界。通过合成算子与近似空间假设将理论与实际稀疏模型联系起来，并在椭圆PDE系数识别和稀疏CT两个逆问题中验证了假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在统计逆学习框架下，研究从有限、含噪、间接观测中恢复稀疏函数的理论性质，建立最优收敛率，并通过实际逆问题验证理论假设的可行性。

Method: 提出ℓ1正则化经验风险最小化器，利用变分源条件刻画源光滑参数r，通过协方差算子的多项式谱衰减定义有效维数指数b，建立非渐近高概率收敛界；利用合成算子分解和近似空间假设验证源条件，并通过滤波Radon变换导出有效维数渐近行为。

Result: 建立了预测范数和ℓ1重建范数下的几乎必然一致性和非渐近高概率收敛率，证明了匹配的极小化最优下界；证明了近似空间k_t中的成员关系等价于最优n项逼近误差的多项式衰减；在椭圆PDE系数识别和稀疏CT两个逆问题中验证了假设并导出了具体收敛率。

Conclusion: ℓ1正则化经验风险最小化器在统计逆学习框架下对于稀疏函数恢复具有最优收敛性质，理论结果可通过合成算子分解和近似空间假设与实际问题有效衔接。

Abstract: We study the recovery of sparse functions from finite, noisy, and indirect observations in the framework of statistical inverse learning. The unknown is modeled as an element of $\ell^1$, and observations are generated through a possibly nonlinear forward operator $A:\ell^1\to H$, where $H$ is a vector-valued reproducing kernel Hilbert space. We propose an $\ell^1$-regularized empirical risk minimizer and develop a theoretical analysis of its statistical properties.
  Under mild assumptions, we establish almost-sure consistency and derive non-asymptotic high-probability convergence rates in both the prediction and $\ell^1$ reconstruction norms. The rates depend on the source smoothness parameter $r$, characterized by a variational source condition, and the effective dimension exponent $b$, describing the polynomial spectral decay of the covariance operator. We further prove matching minimax lower bounds, showing that the obtained convergence rates are optimal.
  To relate the theory to practical sparsity models, we consider finitely smoothing operators of the form $A=G\circ S$, where $S$ is a synthesis operator, and show that approximation-space assumptions imply the required variational source conditions. In particular, we prove that membership in the approximation space $k_t$ is equivalent to polynomial decay of the best $n$-term approximation error.
  Finally, we verify the assumptions for two representative inverse problems: reaction coefficient identification in elliptic PDEs and sparse computed tomography. For filtered Radon transforms, we derive explicit effective-dimension asymptotics, yielding concrete convergence rates for standard image models and sparsifying systems.

</details>


### [147] [Value of Information under Imprecise Probabilities: Decision-Rule-Specific Values and Fixed-Measure Envelopes on a Credal Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06570)
*Rowan Iskandar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在概率测度不确定（即信用集）条件下，信息价值（VOI）分析的两种不同表述：规则特定VOI（如Gamma-maximin）和固定测度包络线，并分析了完全信息、部分信息和样本信息的期望值性质，以及如何通过有限线性规划计算其端点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VOI分析假设单一概率测度，但实际中证据往往只能将测度确定到一个集合（信用集）。因此需要发展在概率测度集下的VOI分析框架。

Method: 提出两种表述：1）规则特定VOI，固定一个决策规则（如Gamma-maximin）衡量信息价值；2）固定测度包络线，在所有可容忍精确测度上评估经典VOI泛函。利用信用集的凸性和有限生成性质，通过线性规划计算上下端点。

Result: 完美信息的期望值在信用集上是凹函数；下端点可由生成测度精确获得，而上端点可能在内部，需通过有限线性规划计算。Gamma-maximin值可能超过整个包络线。建立了连续界以限制VOI随测度变化的幅度。

Conclusion: 规则特定VOI与包络线端点不重合，两者分别区分了跨所有可容忍测度成立的结论与依赖于单一未识别测度选择的结论，为概率测度不确定下的VOI分析提供了严谨框架。

Abstract: Value-of-information (VOI) analysis is usually conducted under a single probability measure. However, in practice, the available evidence often pins the measure down only to a set. Consequently, under a set of probability measures, VOI requires different formulations. First, we explicate a rule-specific VOI that fixes a decision rule for acting under imprecision (such as Gamma-maximin) and measures what the information is worth to a decision maker who uses that rule. Second, we derive a fixed-measure envelope that evaluates the classical VOI functional over all admissible precise measures. We formalize this distinction and explicate its consequences for the expected perfect, partial, and sample information. The expected value of perfect information is concave over the credal set. Hence, when the set is generated by finitely many measures, its lower envelope endpoint is obtained exactly from the generators, while its upper endpoint may be interior and is computed by a finite linear program. The Gamma-maximin value, in contrast, can exceed the entire envelope, so a rule-specific value is not recovered from the envelope's endpoints. A continuity bound limits how much the VOI can change as the measure varies, and we identify when the partial- and sample-information endpoints can still be obtained from the generators. Because the single-measure VOI must itself be estimated, the procedure we give combines standard estimators for it with a search over the credal set. By using a worked decision problem, we show how the two quantities separate conclusions that hold across every admissible measure from conclusions that depend on one unidentified choice of measure.

</details>


### [148] [Fast determinantal sampling on general spaces and diffusion geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06644)
*Hoang-Son Tran,Pranav Gupta,Subhroshekhar Ghosh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了行列式点过程（DPP）在远超已知欧几里得设定（如黎曼流形和加权网络）上的显式采样率保证，实现了与欧几里得空间已知速率相匹配的 (样本量)^{-1/2 - 1/(2d_int)} 的收敛速率，其中 d_int 是内在维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPP作为一种基于核的采样方法，在构建高效小批量、核心集等紧凑数据表示方面显示出优于传统i.i.d.采样的近似特性，且能部署在非常一般的空间上。然而，现有的理论保证主要局限于欧几里得空间，缺乏对更广泛空间（如流形和网络）上DPP采样率的明确刻画。

Method: 利用谱核（从拉普拉斯算子或马尔可夫扩散算子的特征空间中获得），连接Weyl定律，并借助马尔可夫扩散理论、Dirichlet形式以及伪微分算子工具进行分析。

Result: 在紧黎曼流形上，DPP采样率能自动捕捉流形的内在维度 d_int；在k近邻图和加权随机几何图上，也展现出类似的对数据内在维度的改进依赖。整体上实现了 (样本量)^{-1/2 - 1/(2d_int)} 的保证速率，匹配欧几里得空间已知结果。

Conclusion: 将DPP的采样率保证从欧几里得空间推广到黎曼流形和加权网络等更一般空间，且速率依赖内在维度而非嵌入维度，揭示了DPP采样在一般空间上的普适性和最优性。

Abstract: Determinantal point processes have recently emerged as a kernel-based alternative to standard independent sampling for constructing efficient minibatches, coresets, and other compact representations of large-scale datasets. In particular, sampling mechanisms based on DPPs are believed to demonstrate better approximation properties compared to classical i.i.d. samplers, even at the scale of the exponent. One of the key strengths of DPP based samplers is that they can be deployed over very general spaces, in contrast to more classical sampling methods beyond i.i.d. which tend to work in very well-structured settings, principally Euclidean spaces. In this work, we establish explicit rate guarantees for determinantal sampling in spaces that extend far beyond known Euclidean setups, focusing on spectral kernels obtained from eigenspaces of naturally associated Laplacian and other Markov diffusion operators. This includes, in particular, Riemannian manifolds and weighted networks. In determinantal sampling from compact Riemannian manifolds, we establish sampling rates that automatically pick up the intrinsic dimensionality $d_{\text{int}}$ of the underlying manifold. In the setting of networks, we investigate DPP-based samplers on the celebrated k-nearest neighbour graphs, as well as weighted random geometric graphs, and demonstrate a similar improved dependence on the intrinsic dimensionality of the data. Overall, our approach achieves guarantees of $\big(\text{sample size}\big)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2d_{\text{int}}}}$ that match known rates on Euclidean spaces of comparable dimension. In terms of techniques, we connect to the celebrated Weyl's Law for manifold spectra, and leverage tools from the theory of Markov diffusions and Dirichlet forms as well as certain ingredients from the theory of pseudodifferential operators, which could be of independent interest in this area.

</details>


### [149] [Heat-Kernel Entropy Profiles and Geometric Effective Sample Size for Weighted Measures on Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06696)
*Kisung You*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出热核熵剖面（heat-kernel entropy profiles），一种多尺度摘要方法，通过在紧流形上扩散加权原子并跟踪非均匀性，得到几何有效样本量，克服了传统仅基于权重的摘要（如有效样本量）忽略支撑集几何结构的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的权重摘要（如有效样本量）忽略了点分布的几何结构，无法区分邻近粒子与分离粒子的差异，在紧流形上的重要性采样、粒子近似、后验摘要、求积和表示学习等任务中存在局限性。

Method: 利用热核在紧流形上扩散加权原子，通过二阶Rényi熵计算成对热核重叠，得到可计算的几何有效样本量；在球面上展开为球谐能量，恢复均值方向、von Mises-Fisher型和Bingham型摘要。

Result: 证明了单调性、小尺度和大尺度渐近性、确定性权重一致性以及紧无边界流形上自归一化重要性采样的有界比扩展。实验表明，该剖面能揭示对跖、环带、多模态和重复粒子结构，这些是仅基于权重和一阶矩的球面摘要无法捕捉的。

Conclusion: 热核熵剖面为紧流形上的加权点过程提供了多尺度几何感知摘要，有效弥补了传统权重摘要的几何盲区，具有良好的理论性质和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Weighted empirical measures on compact manifolds arise in importance sampling, particle approximations, posterior summaries, quadrature, and representation learning. Standard weight-only summaries, such as ordinary effective sample size, ignore the geometry of the support. We introduce heat-kernel entropy profiles, a multiscale summary that diffuses weighted atoms by intrinsic heat flow and tracks nonuniformity across scales. For order-two Rényi entropy, the profile is computable from pairwise heat-kernel overlaps and yields a geometric effective sample size that discounts nearby or duplicate particles while matching ordinary effective sample size for well-separated particles. We prove monotonicity, small- and large-scale asymptotics, deterministic-weight consistency, and a bounded-ratio self-normalized importance-sampling extension for compact manifolds without boundary. On spheres, the unlogged profile decomposes into spherical-harmonic energies that recover mean-direction, von Mises-Fisher-type, and Bingham-type summaries. Sphere-based experiments show that the profile reveals antipodal, girdle, multimodal, and duplicate-particle structure missed by weight-only and first-moment spherical summaries.

</details>


### [150] [Tensor Train Diffusion: Leveraging Low-Rank Structures for High-Dimensional Score-Based Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06841)
*Robert Gruhlke,Julius Berner,David Sommer,Lorenz Richter*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于函数张量列（FTT）格式的高效求解器，用于扩散模型中反向随机微分方程所对应的HJB方程，通过利用潜在低秩结构实现高维函数的压缩与快速计算，并结合BSDE的逆向迭代方案，实现了快速、鲁棒且精确的采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的HJB方程求解方法（如PINNs或轨迹方法）存在训练时间长、对超参数调优敏感等瓶颈。

Method: 采用函数张量列（FTT）格式利用潜在低秩结构高效近似高维函数，结合基于向后随机微分方程（BSDE）的逆向时间迭代方案。

Result: 克服了现有技术的主要瓶颈，实现了更高效的高保真采样。

Conclusion: FTT格式与BSDE逆向迭代结合的方法是一种快速、鲁棒且精确的扩散模型采样方案，显著提升了效率。

Abstract: Diffusion models offer a powerful framework for sampling from complex probability densities by learning to reverse a noising process. A common approach involves solving for the time-reversed stochastic differential equation (SDE), which requires the score function of the evolving sample distribution. The logarithm of this distribution's density is governed by a Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) type partial differential equation (PDE). However, current methods for solving this PDE, such as PINNs or trajectory-based techniques, often suffer from long training times and significant sensitivity to hyperparameter tuning. In this work, we introduce a novel and efficient solver for the underlying HJB equation based on the functional tensor train (FTT) format. The FTT representation leverages latent low-rank structures to efficiently approximate high-dimensional functions, enabling both model compression and rapid computation. By integrating this efficient representation with a backward-in-time iterative scheme derived from backward stochastic differential equations (BSDEs), we develop a fast, robust and accurate sampling method. Our approach overcomes primary bottlenecks of existing techniques, enabling high-fidelity sampling from challenging target distributions with improved efficiency.

</details>


### [151] [Finding a stationary point of a stochastic convex problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06883)
*Felipe Areces,John Duchi,Malo Sommers*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究随机凸优化中寻找驻点的问题，提出以目标函数次微分包含小元素作为强收敛准则，并利用维数理论分解凸函数次微分图，证明随机采样可保留这些图的"片段"，从而有效应用邻近点类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机凸优化方法通常使用平稳性的替代度量（如接近平稳性的保证或Moreau包络的小梯度），但这些准则不够强。作者希望建立更强的收敛准则——要求目标函数的次微分本身包含小元素，这更具挑战性，因为凸函数的次微分即使在最优点的任意小邻域内也无法一致收敛。

Method: 利用维数理论（dimension theory）来分解凸函数次微分的图（graph），证明随机采样可以保留这些图的"片段"（pieces），从而使得邻近点类方法（proximal-point-like methods）能够有效应用。

Result: 提出了一个基于次微分包含小元素的更强驻点准则，并证明了在该准则下随机凸优化问题的收敛性保证。

Conclusion: 维数理论为分析随机凸优化中次微分的收敛性提供了新工具，使得更强的驻点准则成为可能，为设计更可靠的随机优化算法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider the problem of finding stationary points for stochastic convex optimization problems. Rather than surrogates to stationarity, such as a proximity-to-stationarity guarantee or small gradient of the Moreau envelope, we ask for a stronger notion: that the subdifferential of the objective actually contains a small element. This criterion is non-trivial, because subdifferentials of convex functions fail to converge uniformly, even in arbitrarily small neighborhoods of the optimum. Our convergence guarantees rely on dimension theory to decompose the graph of the subdifferential of a convex function, showing how stochastic sampling preserves "pieces" of these graphs, and allowing effective application of proximal-point-like methods.

</details>


### [152] [Tensorized algorithms and scalable filtering methods for hidden Markov and factorial hidden Markov models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07008)
*Roxana Barrios,Ioannis Sgouralis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于张量代数的可扩展因子隐马尔可夫模型（fHMM）滤波方法，通过直接利用多维结构避免传统HMM等价转换带来的状态空间爆炸问题，显著提升了计算效率，使fHMM能够应用于大规模系统和数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fHMM能更自然地表示受多个独立因素影响的真实系统，但将其转化为等价HMM会导致状态空间指数级增长，前向滤波等核心算法计算成本过高，限制了fHMM在大规模系统中的应用。

Method: 利用张量代数直接操作fHMM的多维结构，避免构建中间HMM表示，开发新型滤波算法以保持数据的张量形式进行高效计算。

Result: 新方法显著提升了计算性能，能够高效分析大规模系统和数据集，使fHMM适用于数据密集型应用。

Conclusion: 张量代数方法有效解决了fHMM的可扩展性问题，扩展了其应用范围，为数据密集型应用提供了实用框架。

Abstract: A common method for the representation and analysis of time-series data is the hidden Markov model (HMM), where each observation is associated with a hidden state that evolves over time. However, many real-world systems are influenced by multiple independent factors, which are more naturally represented by factorial hidden Markov models (fHMM), where several hidden Markov chains jointly generate the observed data. Although an fHMM provides a richer and more realistic representation of many real-world systems, it can be reformulated as an equivalent HMM, but with a significantly larger state-space, leading to a severe increase in computational cost. In particular, the forward filtering algorithm, which is central to evaluation, decoding, and estimation tasks, becomes prohibitively expensive even for small systems. This work focuses on developing scalable methods for time-series analysis using tensor algebra to exploit the multidimensional structure of fHMM directly, without constructing intermediate HMM representations. Our novel filtering approach significantly improves computational performance and enables the efficient analysis of large systems and datasets, extending the scope of fHMM and providing a practical framework for data intensive applications.

</details>


### [153] [DiPhon: Diffusion on Graphons for Scalable Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07232)
*Sergio Rozada,Yiming Qin,Manuel Madeira,Pascal Frossard,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出DiPhon，一种基于图极限对象（graphon）和Jacobi随机微分方程的可扩展图生成扩散框架，能够在训练时使用小图、推理时生成更大规模的图，而无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图扩散模型在生成大图时面临可扩展性问题，尤其是密集图场景下缺乏有效的尺度泛化方法。

Method: 利用图极限对象graphon作为与图大小无关的极限结构，在graphon空间上构建连续的Jacobi SDE扩散过程；提出离散化的图级过程DiPhon来模拟该连续动力学；导出对应的逆时间过程，并利用Jacobi过程的边际得分具有可解析表达的特性，通过图去噪进行估计。

Result: 理论上证明DiPhon精确匹配连续graphon过程的一阶矩，并近似二阶矩（具有闭式差异）；实验上展示从小图训练后可生成更大图且保持核心拓扑性质。

Conclusion: DiPhon通过graphon视角实现了图生成模型在规模上的可扩展性，为大规模图生成提供了理论基础和实用框架。

Abstract: Diffusion models represent a leading paradigm for graph generation, with notable impact in domains such as molecular design. Yet, scaling these models to large graphs remains an open problem. We approach this question in the dense-graph setting through the lens of graphons, the size-agnostic limit objects of dense graph sequences, to study how structural graph statistics behave across node-size scales. This perspective leads to DiPhon, a diffusion framework for size-scalable graph generation. Specifically, we formulate a continuous diffusion process on the graphon space via a Jacobi stochastic differential equation (SDE), and propose DiPhon, a discretized graph-level process that mimics these dynamics on finite graphs. We further derive the corresponding reverse-time process, which requires access to the marginal score. For the Jacobi process, this score interestingly admits a tractable form, which we estimate from data via graph denoising and plug into the reverse process to generate graph samples. We prove that DiPhon matches exactly the first moment of the marginal distributions induced by the continuous graphon process, and approximates the second moment up to a closed-form discrepancy. Thus, DiPhon inherits key size-agnostic statistical properties of the graphon dynamics, providing a principled route toward scalable graph generation. Empirically, we demonstrate this scalability by training on small graphs and generating progressively larger graphs at inference time, without retraining, while preserving their core topological properties.

</details>


### [154] [A Unified Detection Framework for AI-Related Content and Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07527)
*Xifeng Zhang,Tao Hu,Yijie Peng,Wan Tian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于马氏距离分数(MDS)的统一检测框架，用于检测AI生成文本、幻觉、水印和对抗样本，核心在于通过联合最小协方差行列式(MCD)估计器准确刻画正类样本的协方差矩阵，并证明了算法的收敛性和高崩溃点性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术是一把双刃剑，在取得巨大成功的同时也需要有效的监督和监管。检测AI相关内容和伪影是最直接且成本效益最高的方法，因此需要统一的检测框架来应对多种重要场景。

Method: 基于马氏距离分数(MDS)的统一检测框架。核心是通过联合估计方法优化正类样本（如人类生成文本、事实陈述、无水印文本或非对抗样本）深度表示的协方差矩阵估计器，具体包括casewise和cellwise最小协方差行列式(MCD)估计量，并设计了高效优化算法。

Result: 证明了所提出优化算法的收敛性，给出了联合估计器崩溃点的高崩溃点性质定义并证明，实验验证了检测框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于MDS的统一检测框架能够有效应用于LLM生成文本检测、幻觉检测、水印检测和对抗样本检测多个重要场景，为AI内容监管提供了高效且统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is a double-edged sword: while it has achieved remarkable success across a wide range of domains, its deployment also calls for effective oversight and regulation, for which the detection of AI-related content and artifacts is perhaps the most direct and cost-effective approach. To this end, we propose a unified detection framework based on Mahalanobis distance scores (MDS), applicable to several important settings, including the detection of large language model (LLM) generated text, hallucination, watermark, and adversarial examples. A key component of the proposed method is to accurately characterize the positive class--such as human-generated text, factual statements, unwatermarked text, or non-adversarial samples--which requires an efficient and robust estimator of the covariance matrix of deep representations of positive samples before computing the MDS. Since the positive samples typically consist of multiple classes, and these classes may exhibit both homogeneity and heterogeneity, we develop joint estimation methods for both the casewise and cellwise minimum covariance determinant (MCD) estimators. We provide efficient optimization algorithms for both estimators and prove their convergence. We provide a reasonable definition of the breakdown point for the joint estimators and prove their corresponding high breakdown point properties. Empirical evaluations confirm the effectiveness of the proposed detection framework.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Expectation-Maximization algorithm to estimate the forcing parameter of a nonlinear McKean-Vlasov diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06714)
*Eduardo Gutierrez-Turner,Kerlyns Martinez,Hector Olivero*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对一类受湍流动能模型启发的非线性McKean-Vlasov随机微分方程，提出了基于期望最大化框架的强迫参数估计算法，证明了估计的一致性，并通过数值实验验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一类非线性McKean-Vlasov型随机微分方程中强迫参数的估计问题，该方程受瞬时湍流动能模型启发，漂移项依赖于解的期望值的幂次，引入了代数非线性。

Method: 基于期望最大化（Expectation-Maximization）框架提出参数估计算法。

Result: 证明了所提估计方法的一致性，并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的EM框架下的估计算法能够一致地估计McKean-Vlasov型SDE中的强迫参数，数值实验支持了理论结果。

Abstract: In this article, we address the problem of estimating a forcing parameter in a stochastic differential equation inspired by a model that describes instantaneous turbulent kinetic energy. The stochastic differential equation we analyze is of the nonlinear McKean-Vlasov type, where the drift term depends on a power of the expected value of the solution, which also introduces nonlinearity in an algebraic sense. We propose an estimation algorithm based on the Expectation-Maximization framework and show the consistency of our method. We illustrate our findings through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [156] [Best Subset Selection in Linear Regression: Fixed-Design Error Bounds and Insights for Random Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06835)
*Maxim Fedotov*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文在固定设计线性回归下，推导了最优子集选择在精确支持恢复方面的非渐近上界，该上界通过一个确定性子集可分离参数表示，并避免了随机设计分析中出现的全对数组合项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定设计线性回归中，研究最优子集选择精确恢复真实支持的概率上界，理解其与信号噪声比、子集可分离性及样本量的关系，并揭示与随机设计分析在组合复杂度项上的差异。

Method: 推导非渐近上界，通过确定性子集可分离参数衡量真实支持与竞争支持在投影后的可区分性，给出样本量充分阈值（依赖信噪比、子集可分离性及对数因子 ln s + ln(d-s)），并讨论随机设计下全对数组合项如何重新出现。

Result: 得到了最优子集选择失败概率的非渐近上界，该上界不含全对数组合项（随机设计分析中常见），仅含 ln s + ln(d-s) 的对数因子；样本量阈值与信噪比和子集可分离性显式相关。

Conclusion: 固定设计视角下最优子集选择的有效复杂度可能降低（如结构化设计或受限候选子集类），且证明策略揭示了随机设计中组合项出现的机制。

Abstract: We study exact support recovery by best subset selection in linear regression under a fixed design. For a known support size $s$ and ambient dimension $d$, we derive a non-asymptotic upper bound on the probability that best subset selection fails to recover the true support. The bound is expressed through a deterministic subset-separability parameter, which measures how well the true support can be distinguished from competing supports after projection. The result holds for all sample sizes $n$ exceeding a certain sufficient threshold which we state explicitly in terms of the signal-to-noise ratio, the subset separability of the realized design, and a logarithmic factor of order $\ln s + \ln(d - s)$. In contrast to random-design analyses, no full log-combinatorial term over the candidate support class appears. We discuss how such terms may reappear when the design is random and the separability parameter must be controlled uniformly over many competing subsets. The fixed-design formulation and the proof strategy also indicate settings in which the effective complexity of best subset selection may be reduced, for instance, under structured designs or restricted candidate subset classes.

</details>


### [157] [Gaussian comparison above the median](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06874)
*Colin B. Fogarty*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个针对闭凸集的高斯比较不等式，当参考概率至少为1/2时，Loewner序下较小协方差矩阵的高斯分布在闭凸集上赋予的概率不小于较大协方差矩阵。这提供了Anderson定理的单边版本，并在统计上为使用保守协方差估计进行单侧和序约束推断提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Anderson定理是高斯测度比较的经典结果，但其对称性假设限制了在非对称闭凸集上的应用。本文旨在推导一个单边版本的高斯比较不等式，适用于闭凸集且参考概率至少为1/2的情形，以填补理论空白并服务于实际统计推断需求。

Method: 采用理论证明的方法，针对中心化高斯向量，在Loewner序下比较不同协方差矩阵在闭凸集上的概率测度，并引入参考概率至少为1/2的条件作为关键假设。

Result: 证明了当两个协方差矩阵满足Loewner序时，较小协方差矩阵在任意闭凸集上赋予的概率不小于较大协方差矩阵，前提是该闭凸集在较大协方差下的测度至少为1/2。

Conclusion: 该结果是Anderson定理的单边推广，为显著性水平低于1/2时使用保守协方差估计进行单侧和序约束推断提供了理论依据，且不要求检验统计量的接受区域具有对称性。

Abstract: We prove a Gaussian comparison inequality for closed convex sets with reference probability at least 1/2. For centered Gaussian vectors whose covariance matrices are ordered in the Loewner sense, the smaller covariance law assigns at least as much probability as the larger covariance law to every closed convex set with measure at least 1/2 under the larger covariance. This provides a one-sided analogue of Anderson's Theorem for Gaussian measures. As a statistical application, the result justifies one-sided and order-restricted inference using conservative covariance estimators at significance levels below 1/2 for test statistics whose acceptance regions are closed and convex but not necessarily symmetric.

</details>


### [158] [A Study on Cumulative Residual Extropy of Linear Consecutive k-out-of-n:G Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06992)
*Aman Pandey,Chanchal Kundu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了与线性连续k-out-of-n:G系统相关的累积残差外熵，推导了其显式表达式，分析了随机序下的行为，建立了边界和特征结果，引入了动态版本并探讨了与平均残差寿命的关系，开发了非参数估计程序并验证了其一致性，通过蒙特卡洛模拟和真实数据集验证了有限样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性连续k-out-of-n:G系统在可靠性理论和工程应用中具有重要地位，但现有研究对这类系统的累积残差外熵缺乏系统性的理论分析和估计方法。

Method: 推导了累积残差外熵的显式表达式；分析了不同随机序下的行为特征；建立了特征的边界和有意义的结果；引入了动态版本并探讨其与平均残差寿命函数的关系；开发了非参数估计程序并建立了估计量的一致性性质；通过蒙特卡洛模拟和真实数据集进行验证。

Result: 得到了累积残差外熵的显式表达式及其在随机序下的行为特征，建立了多个有意义的边界和特征化结果；所提出的非参数估计量具有一致性，蒙特卡洛模拟和真实数据验证表明有限样本性能良好。

Conclusion: 本文为线性连续k-out-of-n:G系统的累积残差外熵提供了完整的理论框架和实用的非参数估计方法，有助于可靠性工程中系统性能的量化评估。

Abstract: In this article, we investigate the cumulative residual extropy associated with linear consecutive k-out-of-n:G systems, which play an important role in reliability theory and engineering applications. We first derive explicit expressions for the proposed measure and examine the behavior of cumulative residual extropy under a variety of stochastic orderings. In addition, several bounds and meaningful results of the characterization are established. Moreover, we also introduced the dynamic version of the cumulative residual extropy and explored the relationship between the proposed dynamic version and the mean residual life function. Fromaninferentialperspective, wedevelopanonparametricestimationprocedure for the cumulative residual extropy and establish the corresponding consistency properties of the estimator. The finite-sample performance of the proposed estimator is further investigated through extensive Monte Carlo simulation studies under different parametric settings and validated through a real dataset.

</details>


### [159] [Stochastic Inversion of Multivariate Uniform-Distribution-Preserving Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07174)
*Alexander J. McNeil,Johanna G. Nešlehová*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种多元单位立方体变换，该变换的分量变换是分段连续可微且保持均匀分布（udp）的。通过随机化定义了一种随机逆变换以克服udp变换的非单射性质。该逆变换保持服从某copula的随机向量的均匀边际，并针对不同随机化产生不同copula。证明了多元随机逆的copula密度变换结果，并在二元情形中进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决分段连续可微且保持均匀分布的多元变换的非单射问题，并利用随机化逆变换生成不同copula，以扩展copula建模的灵活性。

Method: 采用随机化方法定义随机逆变换，克服udp变换的非单射性；推导并证明多元随机逆变换的copula密度变换公式。

Result: 成功证明了多元随机逆变换的copula密度变换定理，并在二元情形下给出了具体说明和验证。

Conclusion: 随机逆变换为生成多样化copula提供了一种新方法，在保持均匀边际的同时能灵活调整依赖结构。

Abstract: A multivariate transformation of the unit cube with component transformations that are piecewise continuously differentiable and uniform distribution preserving (udp) is considered. A stochastic inverse transformation is defined using randomization to overcome the non-injective nature of the udp transformations. The inverse transformation preserves the uniform margins of a random vector distributed according to a copula and yields different copulas for different randomizations. A copula density transformation result for the multivariate stochastic inverse is proved and illustrated in the bivariate case.

</details>


### [160] [The Randomized BH Procedure: A Generalized Framework Encompassing Conformal and Competition Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07245)
*Mingzhou Deng,Yan Fu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出随机化BH程序（Randomized BH），一个将随机化引入Benjamini-Hochberg过程的通用框架，统一了经典BH方法、竞争检验和共形检验三类方法，并放宽了它们各自的理论假设条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的BH类方法（经典BH、竞争检验、共形检验）虽各有优势，但分别受限于严格的独立性/弱依赖性假设、无零分布假设下的理论分析困难以及严格的交换性假设。作者希望建立一个统一的随机化框架，打破这些限制，使得三类方法能在更宽松的条件下实现FDR控制。

Method: 提出了Randomized BH框架，通过在BH过程中引入随机化机制来构造p值或检验统计量。该框架将经典BH、竞争检验和共形检验作为特例统一起来。具体应用包括：(1) 从随机化视角深入分析复合p值（compound p-values）；(2) 提出一种整合分布式多重检验问题并维护全局FDR控制的通用方法。

Result: 理论结果表明Randomized BH放松了经典FDR控制所需的条件；共形检验不再需要严格的交换性假设，允许通过预筛选构建校准集；竞争检验可以通过成熟的p值理论进行分析。数值模拟验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 随机化BH框架为FDR控制提供了一个统一且更灵活的理论基础，有望在实际应用中降低对数据结构和假设条件的依赖，特别是在分布式检验和复合p值分析等场景中具有重要价值。

Abstract: We propose the Randomized BH procedure, a generalized framework that introduces randomization into the Benjamini-Hochberg (BH) procedure for false discovery rate (FDR) control. We show that the classical BH procedure as well as two recently developed families of null-distribution-free methods, competition tests and conformal tests, share a common theoretical foundation and arise as special cases of this framework. The Randomized BH relaxes the classical conditions required for FDR control, frees conformal tests from the strict exchangeability assumption while allowing calibration sets to be constructed via pre-screening, and enables competition tests to be analyzed through the well-developed theory of p-values. We illustrate the framework through two applications. The first is a deeper analysis of compound p-values through the lens of randomization. The second is a general approach for integrating distributed multiple testing problems while maintaining global FDR control. Numerical simulations confirm the validity of our theoretical results.

</details>


### [161] [Gradient-free stochastic optimization of derivatives under strong convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07249)
*Arya Akhavan,Sirine Louati,Alexandre B. Tsybakov*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于带噪查询估计未知函数g的k阶偏导数f=∂ⱼᵏg，并在f强凸且g具有Hölder正则性条件下，提出了核估计器驱动的投影随机梯度算法，获得了N^{-(β-1)/(β+k)}的最优优化误差率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维优化和统计学习中，经常需要基于带噪观测来优化未知函数的导数，而非函数本身。本文旨在解决如何在仅有g的带噪查询下，高效优化其高阶偏导数f的问题，并建立理论保证。

Method: 假设g具有Hölder正则性β+k（β≥2），f在紧凸集Θ上强凸，且g和f满足有界性和Lipschitz正则性条件。提出基于核方法的梯度估计器，并将其代入投影随机梯度下降算法中，进行非渐近误差分析。

Result: 获得了优化误差的非渐近上界d^{(2β+k-1)/(β+k)} N^{-(β-1)/(β+k)}，并建立了N^{-(β-1)/(β+k)}的极小化最优下界，证明该收敛率在N上对全体序贯算法是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文首次在仅有g的带噪查询条件下，对k阶偏导数的优化问题给出了最优收敛率，填补了该方向的理论空白。

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimizing the $k$-th order partial derivative $f=\partial_j^k g$ of an unknown function $g$ along a fixed coordinate direction $j$, based on noisy queries of $g$. Assuming that $g$ has Hölder regularity ${β+k}$ for some $β\ge 2$, that $f$ is strongly convex on a compact convex set $Θ\subset\mathbb{R}^d$ and that $g$ and $f$ satisfy mild boundedness and Lipschitz regularity conditions on $Θ$, we propose a kernel-based estimator of $\nabla f$ and analyze the projected stochastic gradient algorithm driven by this estimator. We obtain a non-asymptotic upper bound on the optimization error of the order $d^{(2β+k-1)/(β+k)}\,N^{-(β-1)/(β+k)}$, where $N$ is the total number of queries. We also establish a minimax lower bound of the order $N^{-(β-1)/(β+k)}$ showing that this rate is optimal in $N$ over all sequential algorithms.

</details>


### [162] [Empirical Bayes Estimation of the Mean of a Function of the Latent Variable with Applications to the Treatment of Nonresponse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07516)
*Eitan Greenshtein,Ya'acov Ritov*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了非参数经验贝叶斯框架下混合分布线性泛函的估计问题，重点处理部分可识别情况（如复杂抽样中的无响应）。论证了即使混合分布的最大似然估计不唯一，将其半参数最大似然估计应用于泛函估计仍是有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂抽样场景（如存在无响应）中，混合分布往往只能被部分识别，这给线性泛函的估计带来了挑战。需要一种稳健且高效的估计方法。

Method: 采用半参数最大似然估计（Semi-parametric Maximum Likelihood Estimator, SMLE）对混合分布进行估计，然后将目标线性泛函直接应用于该估计量。

Result: 即使混合分布的MLE不唯一，基于半参数MLE的泛函估计仍然是有效的工具。

Conclusion: 半参数最大似然估计可作为部分可识别混合分布下线性泛函估计的可靠方法。

Abstract: We consider the estimation of linear functionals of the mixing distribution in a nonparametric empirical Bayes framework. Our main interest is in situations in which the mixing distribution is only partially identifiable, as may arise in complex sampling situations with nonresponse. We argue that estimating the functional by applying it to the semiparametric maximum likelihood estimator of the mixing distribution is an efficient tool, even when the maximum likelihood estimator is not unique.

</details>


### [163] [Any-Dimensional Learning by Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07680)
*Eitan Levin,Venkat Chandrasekaran*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，利用随机采样映射（包括有放回采样、随机分箱和物种采样）来比较不同规模的输入数据，从而解决机器学习模型在大小未见过的输入上的泛化问题以及大规模输入的草图化近似问题。该框架为序列、图和张量等不同领域中的函数类提供了显式的泛化和草图化速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型常处理不同大小的输入（如点云、序列、图），但训练数据规模有限。需要解决：(1) 模型从小规模输入泛化到未见过的更大规模输入的能力；(2) 如何对大规模输入进行草图化以获得相似模型输出的小规模替代。核心需求是比较不同大小的输入并用小输入近似大输入。

Method: 提出利用随机采样映射（有放回采样、随机分箱、物种采样）作为统一工具来比较不同规模的输入。根据问题域中不同规模实例之间的对称性和关系，刻画各类采样的适用领域。对相对于所选采样概念连续的函数类，推导出显式的泛化速率和草图化速率。

Result: 框架涵盖了定义在序列、图和不同规模张量上的大规模函数族，包括测度上的矩多项式、图同态密度和计数、置换不变Transformer以及图神经网络等具体示例，为这些模型提供了理论上的泛化和草图化保证。

Conclusion: 随机采样映射为比较不同规模输入提供了统一途径，能有效分析模型在大小输入上的泛化行为，并为大规模输入的草图化近似提供理论依据。

Abstract: Many machine learning models are defined for inputs of different sizes, such as point clouds containing different numbers of points, sequences of tokens of different lengths, and graphs on different numbers of nodes. Such models are trained on finitely-many examples of necessarily limited sizes. How well do these models generalize from inputs of small size to larger inputs of size not seen during training? Furthermore, evaluating such models on large inputs is often expensive. How can we sketch large inputs to obtain smaller ones on which the model takes similar values? At the heart of both questions is the need to compare inputs of different sizes and to approximate large inputs by small ones. We present a unified approach to address these questions by using random sampling maps to compare inputs of different sizes. The sampling maps we consider are generalizations of sampling with replacement, random binning, and species sampling. We characterize the application domains in which each type of sampling is appropriate in terms of the symmetries and relations between problem instances of different sizes in the domain. Our framework yields explicit generalization and sketching rates for function classes continuous with respect to a chosen notion of sampling, encompassing large families of functions defined on sequences, graphs, and tensors of different sizes. Specific examples include moment polynomials on measures, homomorphism densities and numbers of graphs, permutation-invariant transformers, and graph neural networks.

</details>


### [164] [Minimum Norm Interpolation via The Local Theory of Banach Spaces: The Role of Gaussianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07694)
*Gil Kur,Reese Pathak*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了过参数化线性回归中最小范数插值（MNI）问题，针对无闭式解的范数（如ℓ₁范数），利用高维几何与概率工具（而非CGMT）推导出偏移界、内在方差上界O(1/(n log(d/n)²))，恢复了ℓ₁-MNI的均方误差界，并在高维几何中改进了各向同性常数比和薄壳估计等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作大多依赖高斯比较工具（如CGMT）分析过参数化线性回归中的MNI，但对于无闭式解的范数（如ℓ₁范数），需要新的理论工具来刻画其统计性质，尤其是均方误差（MSE）的精确界。

Method: (1) 利用高维几何与概率工具，对处于各向同性位置的范数推导出"偏移"界，控制MNI对真实参数的收缩量；(2) 结合Cordero-Erausquin和Ledoux的L₁-L₂不等式变体与Gluskin的经典结果，证明ℓ₁-MNI的内在方差上界为O(1/(n log(d/n)²))；(3) 利用Fleury关于对称高斯多面体的工作恢复ℓ₁-MNI的MSE界，替代CGMT方法。

Result: (1) 获得了ℓ₁-MNI的MSE的sharp bound，与Wang et al. [2022]的结果一致；(2) 以压倒性概率证明对称高斯多面体P_{n,d}的各向同性常数与ℝⁿ中欧几里得球的各向同性常数之比不超过1+O((log(d/n))⁻²)，改进了Klartag和Kozma的结果；(3) 建立了关于P_{n,d}的精炼加权薄壳估计，并提供了Fleury主要定理的初等证明。

Conclusion: 本文展示了一种不依赖CGMT的分析框架，能够有效处理无闭式解的MNI问题，同时在高维几何中产生了具有独立价值的改进结果。

Abstract: We study minimum-norm interpolation (MNI) in overparameterized linear regression with isotropic Gaussian covariates, in settings where the MNI has no closed-form formula. Whereas most prior work relied on Gaussian comparison tools such as the convex Gaussian min--max theorem (CGMT), our approach uses tools from high-dimensional geometry and probability. First, when the norm is in isotropic position, we obtain an ``offset'' bound that controls the amount by which the MNI shrinks the ground truth. Second, we show that the ``intrinsic'' variance of the $\ell_1$-MNI is at most $O(\tfrac{1}{n\log(d/n)^2})$, using a variant of Talagrand's $L_1$--$L_2$ inequality due to Cordero-Erausquin and Ledoux [2012], together with a classical result of Gluskin [1988]. We recover the sharp mean-squared error (MSE) bound for the $\ell_1$-MNI obtained by Wang et al. [2022], using the work of Fleury [2012] on the symmetric Gaussian polytope, which is defined via \[
  P_{n,d} := \mathrm{conv}\{\pm X_i\}_{i=1}^{d} \text{ where }
  X_i \overset{\mathrm{i.i.d.}}{\sim} N(0,\mathrm{I}_{n \times n}), \] rather than CGMT. Our methods also imply improvements on previous results in high-dimensional geometry that may be of independent interest. First, we show that with overwhelming probability, the ratio between the isotropic constant of $P_{n,d}$ and that of the Euclidean ball in $\mathbb{R}^n$ is at most $1+O((\log(d/n))^{-2})$, improving a result of Klartag and Kozma [2009]. We also establish a refined weighted thin-shell estimate on $P_{n,d}$, and provide an elementary proof of the main theorem of Fleury [2012].

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Fast Rates for Semi-Supervised Learning via Data-Augmentation Graph Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07513)
*Adam M. Oberman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为自监督学习的标签效率提供了理论解释：数据增强诱导的相似图使下游学习成为图拉普拉斯正则化学习，证明了一个快速传递率 $O(1/n_L)$（远优于监督学习的 $O(1/\sqrt{n_L})$），并将误差上界分解为 $C/n_L + R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$，其中 $R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$ 是增强对齐误差——跨越标签边界的增强图割质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习在少标签情况下能达到与监督学习相当的精度，但其标签样本效率背后缺乏理论解释。现有分析要么依赖不切实际的假设（精确核、泛化特征），要么仅给出泛化间隙的界而非解释实际准确率与标签数量的关系曲线。

Method: 将数据增强诱导的相似图上的下游学习建模为图拉普拉斯正则化学习；将Johnson和Zhang的留一法稳定性分析框架迁移到增强图上；设计了一种精简损失函数（去除了投影头、负样本和正交性开销），仍能在无限数据极限下恢复top-K理想特征（增强核特征空间）。

Result: 证明了传递率 $O(1/n_L)$ 优于监督学习的 $O(1/\sqrt{n_L})$；误差上界为 $C/n_L + R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$，其中增强对齐误差 $R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$ 量化了增强质量；该界能解释实际准确率与标签数量的关系曲线，而不仅仅是给出泛化间隙的界。

Conclusion: 自监督学习的标签效率源于增强图的正则化结构，良好的数据增强可以显著减少所需标签数量。该理论无需不切实际的假设，为理解和改进自监督学习提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning matches supervised accuracy from a fraction of the labels, but the labeled-sample efficiency behind this has lacked a theoretical explanation. We provide one. Data augmentation induces a similarity graph on the unlabeled data, so downstream learning on that graph is graph-Laplacian-regularized learning. We prove a fast transductive rate, $O(1/n_L)$ in the number of labels, in place of the supervised $O(1/\sqrt{n_L})$, by carrying the leave-one-out stability apparatus of Johnson and Zhang (JMLR 2007) over to the augmentation graph, and without the unrealistic assumptions of limit-based analyses (exact kernel, generalizing features). The bound makes augmentation quality explicit: the expected error is at most $C/n_L + R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$, where the data-augmentation alignment error $R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$ is the graph-cut mass of augmentations that cross a label boundary, so good augmentations let few labels suffice. The analysis uses a streamlined loss that drops the projector, negative-sample, and orthogonality overhead of standard objectives yet still recovers the top-$K$ ideal features in the infinite-data limit, the augmentation-kernel eigenspace studied by Zhai et al. The result explains the observed accuracy-versus-label-count curve rather than only bounding a generalization gap.

</details>


### [166] [Gradient-free Riemannian Langevin Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07519)
*Ricardo Baptista,Olivier Zahm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需梯度的黎曼 Langevin 采样器 (GRiLS)，通过引入黎曼度量重塑局部几何结构，促进多模态分布间的跳转，改善 MCMC 的混合效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 MCMC 在多模态概率分布采样中存在混合不良和模式陷困问题，而现有梯度类方法在复杂、计算昂贵的目标分布中梯度不可用或不实用。

Method: 提出 GRiLS 采样器，通过引入黎曼度量重塑局部几何结构以促进模式间跳转；无需目标密度的梯度评估；需已知目标分布的均值和协方差，利用一组交互粒子进行估计。

Result: 在多模态基准测试中，GRiLS 相比现有基于梯度和无梯度的 MCMC 方法均取得了更优的混合效果。

Conclusion: GRiLS 是一种高效的无梯度 MCMC 方法，特别适用于梯度不可用或不实用的复杂多模态分布采样场景。

Abstract: We address the problem of efficiently sampling multimodal probability distributions, where standard Markov Chain Monte Carlo methods often suffer from poor mixing and mode trapping. To mitigate these issues, we propose Gradient-free Riemannian Langevin Sampler (GRiLS), a novel proposal that improves exploration without requiring gradient evaluations of the target density. Our approach introduces a Riemannian metric which reshapes the local geometry in order to facilitate transitions across modes. The resulting gradient-free MCMC algorithm is particularly suitable for complex, computationally expensive targets where derivatives are unavailable or impractical. The GRiLS proposal requires knowing the mean and covariance of the target density, which we estimate using an ensemble of interacting particles. Empirical results on multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that GRiLS achieves improved mixing compared to existing gradient-based and gradient-free MCMC approaches.

</details>


### [167] [TriRoute: Unified Learned Routing for Joint Adaptive Attention, Experts, and KV-Cache Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06601)
*Andrii Balashov,Olena Ponomarova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TriRoute提出一个统一的轻量级控制器，联合优化注意力模式、专家选择和KV缓存位宽三个轴，在匹配计算和内存成本下，Pareto优于独立的MoD+MoE+KV量化组合，并更好保持对罕见实体、代码和算术的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件计算技术（MoE、MoD、KV缓存量化）各自在单一轴上独立优化，忽略了注意力分辨率、专家选择和缓存位宽之间的强耦合关系，导致无法实现最优的推理效率和模型质量平衡。

Method: 设计一个跨三个轴的轻量级控制器，对每个token每层输出协调策略：(i)注意力模式（跳过/局部/全局），(ii)稀疏FFN专家集（含空专家恢复MoD），(iii)KV缓存位宽。使用异质松弛（Gumbel-Softmax+直通估计用于分类决策，负载均衡top-k门控用于专家）进行端到端训练，并在拉格朗日预算约束下控制平均计算和内存成本。提出跨轴路由崩溃问题，通过逐轴归一化和耦合感知平衡损失解决。

Result: 在1.6亿到13亿参数的decoder-only模型上，TriRoute在匹配推理FLOPs和内存下Pareto优于独立MoD+MoE+KV量化组合，更好保持对罕见实体、代码和算术的尾案例鲁棒性。控制器展现出可解释结构：将全局注意力和高精度缓存分配给句首位置、罕见子词和命名实体，而功能词则使用廉价路由。

Conclusion: 条件计算中多个决策轴存在强耦合关系，联合优化比独立优化更有效，且联合策略具有可解释的语义结构，能够更好保持模型在长尾分布上的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Conditional computation can decouple language model quality from per-token inference cost, yet leading techniques act on a single axis in isolation: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) sparsifies the FFN, Mixture-of-Depths (MoD) skips whole transformer blocks, and KV-cache quantization compresses attention memory. We argue these three decisions (attention resolution, expert selection, and cache bit-width) are strongly coupled and should be made jointly: a token rare enough to warrant full attention may also need high-precision caching regardless of which expert processes it. We introduce TriRoute, a single lightweight controller shared across all three axes that, for every token at every layer, emits a coordinated policy: (i) an attention mode (skip/local/full), (ii) a sparse set of FFN experts (with a null expert recovering MoD), and (iii) a KV-cache bit-width. The controller trains end-to-end via a heterogeneous relaxation (Gumbel-Softmax with straight-through estimation for categorical decisions and load-balanced top-k gating for experts) under a Lagrangian budget constraint that turns the average compute and memory cost into a controllable knob. We identify a cross-axis routing-collapse cascade in naive joint training, where collapse on one axis propagates to the others, and address it with per-axis normalization and a coupling-aware balancing loss. On decoder-only models from 160M to 1.3B parameters at compute-optimal token counts, TriRoute Pareto-dominates the best independent MoD+MoE+KV-quantization combination at matched inference FLOPs and memory, while better preserving tail-case robustness on rare entities, code, and arithmetic that pure perplexity optimization erodes. Post-hoc analysis reveals interpretable structure: the controller allocates full attention and high-precision cache to sentence-initial positions, rare subwords, and named entities, while cheaply routing function words.

</details>


### [168] [A Quiet Failure in Calibrated Virtual Screening: Marginal Conformal Prediction Under-Covers the Minority Class, and a Class-Conditional Fix Recovers It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06605)
*Muhammadjon Tursunbadalov,Mustafojon Tursunbadalov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准共形预测在药物发现中的不平衡数据集上，虽能保证全局90%的覆盖率，但少数类覆盖率严重偏低（低至4.2%），而条件共形预测（Mondrian）能有效修复这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测被用于药物发现以量化模型可靠性，但其在不平衡数据上的表现存在潜在危险，本文旨在揭示并解决这一被忽视的问题。

Method: 在四个药物发现数据集上，使用随机森林、图神经网络和化学语言模型三种模型，对比标准共形预测与Mondrian条件共形预测的覆盖率表现；通过守恒恒等式解释不平衡效应，并引入成本模型量化实际影响。

Result: 标准共形预测在不平衡数据上少数类覆盖率降至64.8%（血脑屏障）和4.2%（临床毒性），该缺陷与模型架构无关；Mondrian方法将少数类覆盖率恢复至目标水平，但预测集大小略有增加。

Conclusion: 标准共形预测在不平衡数据上的全局覆盖具有欺骗性，Mondrian条件共形预测可有效修复，研究提供了诊断指标和实用协议以保障每类可靠性。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is being adopted in drug discovery to put an honest number on model reliability: pick an error rate alpha, and the method returns prediction sets containing the true label with probability at least 1 - alpha. We show this guarantee can be dangerous on imbalanced datasets. Across four datasets, standard (marginal) conformal prediction hits its global 90% coverage target while leaving the minority class badly exposed: realized minority coverage falls to 64.8% on blood-brain-barrier penetration and to 4.2% on clinical-trial toxicity, where the rare class is nearly abandoned. The failure is not tied to one model: a random forest, a graph network, and a frozen chemical language model all reproduce it (p < 0.001 in every case), with severity tracking baseline calibration on rare labels rather than architecture. A conservation identity explains the effect: the minority's shortfall equals the majority's surplus amplified by the imbalance ratio, predicting the measured gap to within one point and ordering severity across datasets. The failure survives realistic scaffold splits and a second conformal score, while aggregate accuracy and overall coverage stay reassuringly high, which is exactly why it is easy to miss. Class-conditional (Mondrian) conformal prediction closes the gap on every dataset, restoring minority coverage to target for a modest increase in prediction-set size. We localize the failures to generic molecular scaffolds - plain benzene and pyridine cores occurring in both classes - propose a one-number diagnostic, and show with a cost model that abstaining on affected compounds flips a screening campaign from net-negative to net-positive utility. Our contribution is demonstrating on real chemistry how severe and invisible this known conformal-theory gap becomes under imbalance, and laying out a practical protocol restoring per-class reliability.

</details>


### [169] [NEST: Tackling Dataset-Level Distribution Shifts via Regime-Oriented Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06607)
*Lanhao Li,Bingshu Xie,Lijun Sun,Xin Xue,Haoyi Zhou,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NEST框架，通过两阶段密集MoE架构对多变量时间序列中的不同运行机制进行建模与重组，在异构网络流量和物理现象等基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注局部时间偏移，未能显式建模数据集由不同运行机制组合而成的全局结构挑战，导致长期预测准确性受损。

Method: NEST采用两阶段密集MoE架构：1）通过无监督聚类在矩-熵空间将数据集划分为不同运行机制；2）引入面向机制的router生成初始专家权重，经几何调制精炼至机制质心；3）每个专家作为专用核函数，通过演化独特的变量注意力模式捕获机制特定动态。

Result: 在异构网络流量和物理现象等多样化基准上持续达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: NEST通过显式建模数据集的全局结构（即不同运行机制的组合）并利用MoE架构进行专业化分解与重组，有效解决了长期预测中的分布偏移问题。

Abstract: Accurate long-term forecasting in complex systems is frequently compromised by dataset-level distribution shifts, where diverse underlying behavioral modes and evolving system states drive the dynamic multivariate time-series. While existing methods predominantly focus on local temporal shifts, they fail to explicitly model the global structural challenge where datasets are composites of distinct operational regimes. In this paper, we propose NEST, a specialized framework designed to model and recompose these evolving structures through a two-phase dense MoE architecture. NEST first facilitates structural specialization by partitioning the dataset into distinct operational regimes through unsupervised clustering in a principled moment-entropy space. We introduce a regime-oriented router mechanism that generates initial expert weights based on temporal content, subsequently refined through geometric modulation to regime centroids.
